# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- request models


class PickRequest(BaseModel):
    player_id: str
    pick_no: int | None = None
    team_slot: int | None = None
    raw_query: str = ""


class StubRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str
    pos: str
    pick_no: int | None = None
    team_slot: int | None = None


class CorrectRequest(BaseModel):
    pick_no: int
    player_id: str | None = None
    stub_name: str | None = None
    stub_pos: str | None = None


class VoidRequest(BaseModel):
    pick_no: int


class ClockRequest(BaseModel):
    pick_no: int


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- serialization helpers


def _team_label(slot: int, my_slot: int) -> str:
    """UNVERIFIED: no manager names exist until Yahoo league settings land, so opponents are
    labeled by draft slot. Replace with real manager names once available (CLAUDE.md)."""
    return "YOU" if slot == my_slot else f"Team {slot}"


def _open_slots_summary(fill: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    """One-line 'what does this team still need' summary, e.g. 'QB done · needs 1 WR · flex
    open' -- the derived line the opponent roster cards render so Marc doesn't have to do the
    arithmetic himself off a bare position-count grid."""
    parts: list[str] = []
    for pos, s in fill["starters"].items():
        if s["need"] <= 0:
            continue
        if s["filled"] >= s["need"]:
            parts.append(f"{pos} done")
        else:
            parts.append(f"needs {s['need'] - s['filled']} {pos}")
    if fill["flex"]["need"] > 0:
        parts.append("flex open" if fill["flex"]["filled"] < fill["flex"]["need"] else "flex filled")
    return " · ".join(parts) if parts else "starters set"


def _pick_view(p, pool: dict[str, live_data.PoolPlayer]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if p.player_id is not None:
        player = pool.get(p.player_id)
        name = player.name if player else p.player_id
        pos = player.pos if player else None
        team = player.team if player else None
    else:
        name = p.stub_name
        pos = p.stub_pos
        team = None
    return {
        "pick_no": p.pick_no,
        "pick_label": None,  # filled in by caller, which knows `teams`
        "team_slot": p.team_slot,
        "player_id": p.player_id,
        "name": name,
        "pos": pos,
        "team": team,
        "is_stub": p.player_id is None,
        "voided": p.voided,
        "out_of_order": p.out_of_order,
    }


class DraftBoard:
    """Holds everything the server needs beyond the raw event log: config, session, pool."""

    def __init__(self, *, cfg: LeagueConfig, my_slot: int, session: DraftSession, pool: list[live_data.PoolPlayer]):
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.my_slot = my_slot
        self.session = session
        self.pool = pool
        self.pool_by_id = live_data.index_by_id(pool)
        self.searchable = live_data.to_searchable(pool)
        self.pos_of = live_data.pos_of_map(pool)
        self.board_players = _build_board_players(pool)

    @property
    def state(self) -> DraftState:
        return self.session.state

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- board view

    def upcoming_picks(self, n: int = 16) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        st = self.state
        out = []
        for offset in range(n):
            pick_no = st.current_pick + offset
            slot = snake.slot_on_clock(self.cfg.teams, pick_no)
            existing = st.picks.get(pick_no)
            out.append(
                {
                    "pick_no": pick_no,
                    "pick_label": snake.pick_label(self.cfg.teams, pick_no),
                    "team_slot": slot,
                    "team_label": _team_label(slot, self.my_slot),
                    "is_mine": slot == self.my_slot,
                    "is_on_clock": offset == 0,
                    "filled": bool(existing and existing.is_filled),
                }
            )
        return out

    def my_upcoming_offsets(self) -> list[int]:
        """Picks-until-mine for each of my future picks in `upcoming_picks`, e.g. [0, 7, 17]."""
        st = self.state
        return [
            pick["pick_no"] - st.current_pick for pick in self.upcoming_picks() if pick["is_mine"]
        ]

    def roster_view(self, team_slot: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        out = []
        for p in self.state.roster(team_slot):
            view = _pick_view(p, self.pool_by_id)
            view["pick_label"] = snake.pick_label(self.cfg.teams, p.pick_no)
            out.append(view)
        return out

    def starter_fill(self, team_slot: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """How many of each starter slot (plus flex) are filled for one team."""
        counts = self.state.roster_positions(team_slot, self.pos_of)
        starters: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {}
        leftover: dict[str, int] = {}
        for pos, need in self.cfg.starters.items():
            have = counts.get(pos, 0)
            filled = min(have, need)
            starters[pos] = {"filled": filled, "need": need}
            leftover[pos] = have - filled
        flex_pool = sum(leftover.get(pos, 0) for pos in self.cfg.flex_eligible)
        flex_filled = min(flex_pool, self.cfg.flex_slots)
        return {
            "starters": starters,
            "flex": {"filled": flex_filled, "need": self.cfg.flex_slots},
            "bench_used": max(0, sum(counts.values()) - sum(s["filled"] for s in starters.values()) - flex_filled),
            "bench_size": self.cfg.bench,
        }

    def opponent_grid(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Positional counts (unchanged) PLUS, per team, the actual roster by name -- the
        biggest gap in the pre-existing payload: a player drafted by another team updated their
        position count but never appeared anywhere by name, and write-ins (not in self.pool)
        couldn't be shown by tier_board either. `roster_view` already resolves both real
        players and stub write-ins; reusing it here is what closes that gap."""
        out = []
        for slot in range(1, self.cfg.teams + 1):
            counts = self.state.roster_positions(slot, self.pos_of)
            unfilled = self.state.unfilled_starters(slot, dict(self.cfg.starters), self.pos_of)
            fill = self.starter_fill(slot)
            qb_fill = fill["starters"].get("QB", {"filled": 0, "need": 0})
            out.append(
                {
                    "team_slot": slot,
                    "team_label": _team_label(slot, self.my_slot),
                    "is_mine": slot == self.my_slot,
                    "counts": counts,
                    "qb_count": counts.get("QB", 0),
                    "qb_unfilled": unfilled.get("QB", 0),
                    "unfilled": unfilled,
                    "starter_fill": fill,
                    "qb_complete": qb_fill.get("filled", 0) >= qb_fill.get("need", 0) > 0,
                    "roster": self.roster_view(slot),
                    "open_slots_summary": _open_slots_summary(fill),
                }
            )
        return out

    def demand_clock(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Per-position supply vs. demand before Marc's own next turn.

        Tone contract (CLAUDE.md): informs, never recommends -- no position here is ranked,
        ordered, or suggested, just the numbers Marc would otherwise have to count in his head
        mid-conversation. `startable_remaining` mirrors the tier board's own ranked-only
        convention: unranked players (value 0.0, `is_ranked=False`) never count as startable
        supply, because a value of 0.0 for them is "no projection", not "worthless".
        """
        st = self.state
        ctx = st.turn_context()
        picks_before_next = (
            max(0, ctx.next_pick - st.current_pick) if ctx.next_pick is not None else 0
        )
        slots_before: list[int] = []
        for pick in range(st.current_pick, st.current_pick + picks_before_next):
            slot = snake.slot_on_clock(self.cfg.teams, pick)
            if slot not in slots_before:
                slots_before.append(slot)

        starters = dict(self.cfg.starters)
        drafted_ids = st.drafted_player_ids
        out: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
        for pos in sorted(self.cfg.positions):
            startable_remaining = sum(
                1
                for p in self.pool
                if p.pos == pos and p.is_ranked and p.value > 0 and p.player_id not in drafted_ids
            )
            league_demand_remaining = sum(
                st.unfilled_starters(t, starters, self.pos_of).get(pos, 0)
                for t in range(1, self.cfg.teams + 1)
            )
            teams_needing_before_next_turn = sum(
                1 for slot in slots_before if pos in st.unfilled_starters(slot, starters, self.pos_of)
            )
            out[pos] = {
                "position": pos,
                "startable_remaining": startable_remaining,
                "league_demand_remaining": league_demand_remaining,
                "teams_needing_before_next_turn": teams_needing_before_next_turn,
                "picks_before_next_turn": picks_before_next,
                # Negative = more unfilled league demand than startable supply left -- the same
                # shut-out condition draftroom.draft.recommend's guardrail 2 watches per-candidate,
                # shown here at the board level for every position, not just the one being picked.
                "cushion": startable_remaining - league_demand_remaining,
            }
        return out

    def tier_board(self) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
        """Every position's players in ADP order, with tier index for the undrafted ones.

        Tiers are computed on the remaining (undrafted) pool only -- "a tier is defined by
        who's left" (CLAUDE.md). Drafted players still appear (struck through in the UI, with
        their owning team) so the board reads as a full depth chart, not just what's left.
        """
        from draftroom.tiers.dynamic import largest_gap_tiers

        drafted_ids = self.state.drafted_player_ids
        owner_by_id: dict[str, int] = {}
        for p in self.state.picks.values():
            if p.is_filled and p.player_id is not None:
                owner_by_id[p.player_id] = p.team_slot

        by_pos: dict[str, list[live_data.PoolPlayer]] = {}
        for p in self.pool:
            by_pos.setdefault(p.pos, []).append(p)

        out: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {}
        for pos, players in by_pos.items():
            # Only RANKED players get tiered. The pool also carries roster-only players with
            # no projection (value 0.0) so they can be recorded; feeding those to the tier
            # engine would bury the real tiers under one huge flat block of zeros and imply
            # we had evaluated them. They come back with tier=None instead.
            available = [
                p for p in players
                if p.player_id not in drafted_ids and getattr(p, "is_ranked", True)
            ]
            tier_of: dict[str, int] = {}
            if available:
                # largest_gap_tiers reads a `dv`/`draft_value` field (it's written against the
                # real valuation module's DraftValue); PoolPlayer's field is `value`, so wrap.
                dv_wrapped = [{"player_id": p.player_id, "dv": p.value} for p in available]
                for tier in largest_gap_tiers(dv_wrapped):
                    for member in tier.members:
                        tier_of[member["player_id"]] = tier.tier
            rows = []
            for p in sorted(players, key=lambda x: x.adp):
                drafted = p.player_id in drafted_ids
                rows.append(
                    {
                        "player_id": p.player_id,
                        "name": p.name,
                        "team": p.team,
                        "bye": p.bye,
                        "adp": p.adp,
                        "value": p.value,
                        # False = roster-only, no projection. The UI must render these as
                        # "no projection" rather than as a player valued at zero.
                        "is_ranked": getattr(p, "is_ranked", True),
                        "drafted": drafted,
                        "owner_team_slot": owner_by_id.get(p.player_id),
                        "owner_label": (
                            _team_label(owner_by_id[p.player_id], self.my_slot)
                            if p.player_id in owner_by_id
                            else None
                        ),
                        "tier": tier_of.get(p.player_id),
                        # Flag badges (informational only -- never alter value/tier/ranking).
                        "sigma_ppg": getattr(p, "sigma_ppg", None),
                        # Danger signal only: True means the sources disagree a lot. False/None
                        # is NOT a safety signal -- see draftroom.valuation.disagreement.
                        "disagreement_high": getattr(p, "disagreement_high", False),
                        "injury_status": getattr(p, "injury_status", None),
                    }
                )
            out[pos] = rows
        return out

    # ------------------------------------------------------------- full payload

    def state_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        st = self.state
        ctx = st.turn_context()
        return {
            "teams": self.cfg.teams,
            "rounds": self.session.rounds,
            "my_slot": self.my_slot,
            # True when nobody told us the slot and we fell back to 1. The UI must surface this;
            # a silently-assumed slot poisons every turn-dependent number on the page.
            "slot_assumed": getattr(self, "slot_assumed", False),
            "current_pick": st.current_pick,
            "current_pick_label": snake.pick_label(self.cfg.teams, st.current_pick),
            "slot_on_clock": st.slot_on_clock,
            "is_my_pick": st.is_my_pick,
            "next_pick": ctx.next_pick,
            "gap_to_next": ctx.gap_to_next,
            "at_the_turn": ctx.at_the_turn,
            "gaps": st.gaps(),
            "upcoming_picks": self.upcoming_picks(),
            "my_roster": self.roster_view(self.my_slot),
            "my_starter_fill": self.starter_fill(self.my_slot),
            "opponents": self.opponent_grid(),
            "tier_board": self.tier_board(),
            "demand_clock": self.demand_clock(),
            "elite_qb_rank_cutoff_default": _DEFAULT_ELITE_QB_CUTOFF,
            "value_note": live_data.PLACEHOLDER_VALUE_NOTE,
        }


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- recommendation


def _build_board_players(pool: list[live_data.PoolPlayer]) -> list[Any] | None:
    """Convert the live pool to `recommend.BoardPlayer`, if that module is importable.

    `BoardPlayer.dv` is meant to carry a real risk-adjusted draft value (their docstring:
    typically `valuation.evob.DraftValue.dv`). Until that pipeline is wired into this server,
    `PoolPlayer.value` -- itself an explicitly-labeled ADP-derived placeholder -- is passed
    through as `dv`, exactly the "SYNTHETIC stand-in derived from ADP" their own module already
    documents and expects to see before real projections land.
    """
    if _recommend_mod is None:
        return None
    try:
        return [
            _recommend_mod.BoardPlayer(
                player_id=p.player_id,
                name=p.name,
                pos=p.pos,
                team=p.team,
                bye=p.bye,
                adp=p.adp,
                stdev=p.stdev,
                dv=p.value,
                dv_sd=0.0,
            )
            for p in pool
        ]
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - defensive, see module docstring
        log.warning("could not build BoardPlayer list for recommend.recommend(): %s", exc)
        return None


def _placeholder_recommendation(board: DraftBoard, pick_no: int) -> Recommendation:
    slot = snake.slot_on_clock(board.cfg.teams, pick_no)
    return Recommendation(
        pick_no=pick_no,
        pick_label=snake.pick_label(board.cfg.teams, pick_no),
        on_the_clock=slot,
        is_my_pick=slot == board.my_slot,
        candidates=(),
        warnings=(
            "Recommendation engine (draftroom.draft.recommend) is not available yet -- "
            "showing an empty placeholder. Search and the tier board are live.",
        ),
    )


def _call_recommend_engine(
    board: DraftBoard, *, elite_qb_rank_cutoff: int = _DEFAULT_ELITE_QB_CUTOFF
) -> Recommendation | None:
    """Call the real recommendation engine if it's importable and the pool converted cleanly.

    `recommend.recommend(state, cfg, players)` always answers for whoever is on the clock
    *right now* (`state.current_pick`) -- it has no pick-number parameter, and itself returns
    an explicit "Not on the clock" placeholder when `state.is_my_pick` is False. So there is no
    real per-pick target to forward here; `target=mine` vs `target=clock` is purely a client-
    side label until it's my turn, at which point they agree. Any failure at all (missing
    module, a partially-built one, an unexpected exception mid-computation) falls back to our
    own placeholder rather than a 500 -- see module docstring.

    `elite_qb_rank_cutoff` is fix "C"(b)'s visible knob (CLAUDE.md/task spec): the UI exposes it
    as a control rather than hardcoding the spec default, so Marc can dial it to 0 (off) or wider
    live if the room's QB run looks different from what the backtest assumed.
    """
    if _recommend_mod is None or board.board_players is None:
        return None
    try:
        result = _recommend_mod.recommend(
            board.state, board.cfg, board.board_players, elite_qb_rank_cutoff=elite_qb_rank_cutoff
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - defensive, see module docstring
        log.warning("recommend.recommend() raised, falling back to placeholder: %s", exc)
        return None
    return result if isinstance(result, Recommendation) else None


def _recommendation_payload(
    board: DraftBoard, pick_no: int, *, elite_qb_rank_cutoff: int = _DEFAULT_ELITE_QB_CUTOFF
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rec = _call_recommend_engine(board, elite_qb_rank_cutoff=elite_qb_rank_cutoff) or (
        _placeholder_recommendation(board, pick_no)
    )
    return dataclasses.asdict(rec)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- app factory


class ConnectionManager:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.active: list[WebSocket] = []

    async def connect(self, ws: WebSocket) -> None:
        await ws.accept()
        self.active.append(ws)

    def disconnect(self, ws: WebSocket) -> None:
        if ws in self.active:
            self.active.remove(ws)

    async def broadcast_json(self, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        dead = []
        for ws in self.active:
            try:
                await ws.send_text(json.dumps(payload))
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a dropped client shouldn't break the pick path
                dead.append(ws)
        for ws in dead:
            self.disconnect(ws)


def create_app(
    *,
    cfg: LeagueConfig | None = None,
    my_slot: int | None = None,
    log_path: Path | str | None = None,
    pool: list[live_data.PoolPlayer] | None = None,
    static_dir: Path | None = None,
) -> FastAPI:
    """Build the FastAPI app. Kept as a factory so tests can inject an isolated event log
    and a deterministic player pool instead of touching the real draft-night files."""
    cfg = cfg or LeagueConfig.from_yaml()
    # The slot is drawn at/near draft night, so it is legitimately unknown during prep. What is NOT
    # acceptable is silently pretending it is 1: every turn-dependent number (survival to your next
    # pick, gaps, VONA, upcoming picks) would be confidently wrong with nothing on screen saying so.
    # Prep tolerates an unknown slot but must ANNOUNCE the assumption; draft night refuses outright
    # (see main()).
    slot_assumed = my_slot is None and cfg.draft_slot is None
    my_slot = my_slot if my_slot is not None else (cfg.draft_slot or 1)
    if slot_assumed:
        log.warning(
            "DRAFT SLOT UNKNOWN -- assuming slot 1. Every turn-dependent number (survival to your "
            "next pick, gaps, VONA) is provisional. Set draft_slot in data/league_manual.yaml or "
            "pass --my-slot once the draw is known."
        )
    log_path = Path(log_path) if log_path is not None else DEFAULT_LOG_PATH
    pool = pool if pool is not None else live_data.load_player_pool()

    session = DraftSession(EventLog(log_path), teams=cfg.teams, rounds=cfg.roster_size, my_slot=my_slot)
    board = DraftBoard(cfg=cfg, my_slot=my_slot, session=session, pool=pool)
    board.slot_assumed = slot_assumed
    manager = ConnectionManager()

    app = FastAPI(title="draftroom")
    app.state.board = board
    app.state.manager = manager

    async def _broadcast() -> None:
        await manager.broadcast_json(board.state_payload())

    @app.get("/healthz")
    def healthz() -> dict[str, str]:
        return {"status": "ok"}

    @app.get("/api/state")
    def api_state() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return board.state_payload()

    @app.get("/api/search")
    def api_search(q: str = "", limit: int = 8, include_drafted: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
        matches = run_search(
            q,
            board.searchable,
            drafted=board.state.drafted_player_ids,
            limit=limit,
            include_drafted=include_drafted,
        )
        return {
            "query": q,
            "matches": [
                {
                    "player_id": m.player.player_id,
                    "name": m.player.name,
                    "pos": m.player.pos,
                    "team": m.player.team,
                    "overall_rank": m.player.overall_rank,
                    "score": m.score,
                    "reason": m.reason,
                    "drafted": m.player.player_id in board.state.drafted_player_ids,
                    # False = roster-only write-in target with no real projection. The UI must
                    # never imply this player was evaluated at zero.
                    "is_ranked": m.player.is_ranked,
                }
                for m in matches
            ],
        }

    @app.post("/api/pick")
    async def api_pick(req: PickRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if req.player_id in board.state.drafted_player_ids:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=f"{req.player_id} is already drafted")
        board.session.record_pick(
            req.player_id, pick_no=req.pick_no, team_slot=req.team_slot, raw_query=req.raw_query
        )
        await _broadcast()
        return board.state_payload()

    @app.post("/api/stub")
    async def api_stub(req: StubRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        board.session.record_stub(req.name, req.pos.upper(), pick_no=req.pick_no, team_slot=req.team_slot)
        await _broadcast()
        return board.state_payload()

    @app.post("/api/undo")
    async def api_undo() -> dict[str, Any]:
        board.session.undo_last()
        await _broadcast()
        return board.state_payload()

    @app.post("/api/correct")
    async def api_correct(req: CorrectRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        board.session.correct_pick(
            req.pick_no, player_id=req.player_id, stub_name=req.stub_name, stub_pos=req.stub_pos
        )
        await _broadcast()
        return board.state_payload()

    @app.post("/api/void")
    async def api_void(req: VoidRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        board.session.void_pick(req.pick_no)
        await _broadcast()
        return board.state_payload()

    @app.post("/api/clock")
    async def api_clock(req: ClockRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        board.session.set_clock(req.pick_no)
        await _broadcast()
        return board.state_payload()

    @app.get("/api/recommendation")
    def api_recommendation(
        target: str = "clock", elite_qb_rank_cutoff: int = _DEFAULT_ELITE_QB_CUTOFF
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if target == "mine":
            ctx = board.state.turn_context()
            pick_no = ctx.next_pick if ctx.next_pick is not None else board.state.current_pick
        else:
            pick_no = board.state.current_pick
        return _recommendation_payload(board, pick_no, elite_qb_rank_cutoff=elite_qb_rank_cutoff)

    @app.websocket("/ws")
    async def ws_endpoint(ws: WebSocket) -> None:
        await manager.connect(ws)
        try:
            await ws.send_text(json.dumps(board.state_payload()))
            while True:
                # The UI never needs to send anything over this socket; it exists purely to
                # push state after every mutation (a second "projected board" window). Just
                # keep the connection alive until the client disconnects.
                await ws.receive_text()
        except WebSocketDisconnect:
            manager.disconnect(ws)

    static_dir = static_dir or (Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "static")
    if static_dir.exists():
        app.mount("/", StaticFiles(directory=str(static_dir), html=True), name="static")

    return app


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- CLI


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m draftroom.server")
    parser.add_argument("--draft", action="store_true", help="Run in offline draft-night mode.")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=DEFAULT_PORT)
    parser.add_argument("--my-slot", type=int, default=None, help="Override the draft slot.")
    parser.add_argument("--log-path", type=str, default=None)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)

    cfg = LeagueConfig.from_yaml()

    # Checked BEFORE the socket guard is installed, so a refusal exits with the process in a clean
    # state rather than leaving a patched socket module behind.
    #
    # Draft night is the one mode where an unknown slot is unacceptable: the board becomes the only
    # record of the draft, and every turn calculation depends on where we sit in the snake. Refuse to
    # start rather than run a whole draft off a silently-assumed slot 1.
    if args.draft and args.my_slot is None and cfg.draft_slot is None:
        log.error(
            "REFUSING TO START DRAFT MODE: draft slot unknown.\n"
            "  The slot is drawn at draft night, so this is expected until then -- but every turn "
            "number depends on it.\n"
            "  Fix either way:\n"
            "    python -m draftroom.server --draft --port 8484 --my-slot 7\n"
            "  or set  draft_slot: 7  in data/league_manual.yaml"
        )
        return 2

    if args.draft:
        install_socket_guard()
        assert_socket_guard_blocks_external()
        log.info("Socket guard installed and verified: outbound non-localhost connections blocked.")

    app = create_app(
        cfg=cfg,
        my_slot=args.my_slot,
        log_path=Path(args.log_path) if args.log_path else None,
    )

    import uvicorn

    uvicorn.run(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=args.port)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command 'cmd /c findstr /n ''^''^ backend'"\\draftroom\\draft\\recommend.py" in C:\dev\draftroom
 succeeded in 3551ms:
1:"""The recommendation engine -- turns a board + draft state into a ranked, explained pick.
2:
3:This is where every other live-draft module gets consumed at once: survival
4:(:mod:`draftroom.draft.survival`) for "is he still there", VONA (:mod:`draftroom.draft.vona`)
5:for "what does waiting on this position cost", tiers (:mod:`draftroom.tiers.dynamic`) for
6:"is this a cliff", the opponent model (:mod:`draftroom.draft.opponents`) and its Monte Carlo
7:roll-forward (:mod:`draftroom.draft.simulate`) for "what will actually be left", and the
8:explain layer (:mod:`draftroom.explain`) to turn all of that into bullets Marc can read in
9:four seconds.
10:
11:**Draft values are synthetic wherever this module is exercised without real projections**
12:(tests, the demo tool) -- see ``tools/demo_recommendation.py`` and ``tests/test_recommend.py``
13:for exactly how, and every synthetic figure is labeled as such at the point it's produced. This
14:module itself does not care where ``BoardPlayer.dv`` came from; it only consumes it.
15:
16:Two design choices worth stating up front:
17:
18:1. **Guardrails exclude, they do not penalize.** A candidate that would leave a starter slot
19:   unfillable, or bust a roster cap, never reaches the ranked list -- it is removed from the
20:   candidate pool before utility is even computed. A "penalty" large enough to always sink such
21:   a candidate is just an exclusion with extra steps and a bug waiting for the day the penalty
22:   isn't large enough.
23:2. **One shared Monte Carlo roll-forward, not one per candidate.** Every candidate's expected
24:   continuation value is read off the SAME set of simulated trials
25:   (:func:`draftroom.draft.simulate.simulate_forward`, called once), filtering out whichever
26:   player the candidate would have taken. The alternative -- resimulating from scratch for each
27:   of a dozen-plus candidates -- would multiply the Monte Carlo cost for a correction (how much
28:   one specific player's absence changes ~11 opponents' aggregate behavior across ~200 other
29:   players) that is second-order. This is a documented approximation, not exact conditioning.

65:
66:#: Default risk-aversion coefficient in `U = E[value] - lam*SD[value]` (spec default).
67:DEFAULT_LAM = 0.25
68:
69:#: Guardrail 2 threshold: fire the CRITICAL shut-out warning once P(startable < demand) exceeds
70:#: this, over the shared Monte Carlo trials. Spec value: 0.30.
71:SHUTOUT_PROB_THRESHOLD = 0.30
72:
73:#: Fix "C" (b), the opportunistic elite grab: with zero QBs rostered, a QB ranked at or above
74:#: this AMONG QBS on the full preseason board (by dv) is ranked first when available -- and a
75:#: QB below it is never reached for by this rule. This is the "visible knob" from the approved
76:#: spec (default top-3); `recommend(elite_qb_rank_cutoff=...)` overrides it per call, and 0
77:#: disables the rule outright. Validated in the 2025 backtest at +16-18 points (t=3.7-4.1)
78:#: (`tools/strategy_tournament.py`, `qb_one_elite_one_cheap`).
79:ELITE_QB_RANK_CUTOFF = 3
80:
81:#: At-the-turn pair optimizer: only pair-partners with at least this much survival odds are
82:#: considered (spec value). Below it, the pair math would be dominated by a coin flip rather
83:#: than a real plan.
84:PAIR_SURVIVAL_FLOOR = 0.85

87:@dataclass(frozen=True)
88:class BoardPlayer:
89:    """One player as the recommendation engine needs to see them: ADP + a draft value.
90:
91:    ``dv`` (and ``dv_sd``) are expected to already be risk-adjusted draft values -- typically
92:    :class:`draftroom.valuation.evob.DraftValue.dv` -- or, until real projections are wired up,
93:    a clearly-labeled SYNTHETIC stand-in derived from ADP (see ``tools/demo_recommendation.py``).
94:    This dataclass is intentionally duck-type-compatible with what
95:    :mod:`draftroom.draft.survival`, :mod:`draftroom.draft.vona`, and
96:    :mod:`draftroom.tiers.dynamic` already know how to read (``.adp``/``.stdev``, ``.pos``,
97:    ``.dv``, ``.player_id``) -- nothing here is a new protocol, it is the union of the fields
98:    those modules already expect on "anything player-ish".
99:    """
100:
101:    player_id: str
102:    name: str
103:    pos: str
104:    team: str
105:    bye: int | None
106:    adp: float
107:    stdev: float | None
108:    dv: float
109:    dv_sd: float = 0.0
110:
111:    @property
112:    def floor(self) -> float:
113:        return self.dv - self.dv_sd
114:
115:    @property
116:    def ceiling(self) -> float:
117:        return self.dv + self.dv_sd

120:# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- guardrails
121:
122:
123:def _feasible_after_pick(
124:    state: DraftState, cfg: LeagueConfig, pos_of: Mapping[str, str], taken_pos: str
125:) -> bool:
126:    """Guardrail 1: after hypothetically taking one more player at `taken_pos`, can Marc still
127:    fill every mandatory starter (+flex) slot with the picks he'd have left? EXCLUDES, never
128:    just down-ranks, a candidate that fails this."""
129:    have = dict(state.roster_positions(state.my_slot, pos_of))
130:    have[taken_pos] = have.get(taken_pos, 0) + 1
131:    unfilled = opp.unfilled_starters_from_counts(have, cfg)
132:    flex_deficit = opp.flex_deficit_from_counts(have, cfg)
133:    total_needed = sum(unfilled.values()) + flex_deficit
134:    remaining_after = state.picks_remaining_for(state.my_slot) - 1
135:    return remaining_after >= total_needed
136:
137:
138:#: UNVERIFIED TUNING CONSTANT: LeagueConfig has no explicit "max at position" field, so this
139:#: encodes the spec's own example ("never a 4th QB in a 2-QB league") as a rule: one bench
140:#: backup beyond the required starters at a non-flex position (2 + 1 = 3, so a 4th is capped
141:#: out); flex-eligible positions get a little more room to stash (flex_slots plus two bench
142:#: spots) because a RB/WR/TE surplus is a normal, useful hedge in a way a 3rd/4th QB isn't.
143:_BENCH_BACKUP_NON_FLEX = 1
144:_BENCH_STASH_FLEX = 2
145:
146:
147:def _max_roster_at_position(cfg: LeagueConfig, pos: str) -> int:
148:    starters_here = cfg.starters.get(pos, 0)
149:    if pos in cfg.flex_eligible:
150:        return starters_here + cfg.flex_slots + _BENCH_STASH_FLEX
151:    return starters_here + _BENCH_BACKUP_NON_FLEX
152:
153:
154:def _roster_cap_ok(
155:    state: DraftState, cfg: LeagueConfig, pos_of: Mapping[str, str], pos: str
156:) -> bool:
157:    """Guardrail 3: roster caps from config. EXCLUDES a candidate that would bust the cap."""
158:    have = state.roster_positions(state.my_slot, pos_of).get(pos, 0)
159:    return have < _max_roster_at_position(cfg, pos)

162:# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- run history
163:
164:
165:def _build_run_detector(
166:    state: DraftState, by_id: Mapping[str, Any], pos_of: Mapping[str, str]
167:) -> PositionalRun:
168:    """Replay the picks already made so the run detector's history matches the real board,
169:    rather than starting a live recommendation from a blank slate."""
170:    run = PositionalRun()
171:    ordered = sorted((p for p in state.picks.values() if p.is_filled), key=lambda p: p.pick_no)
172:    pool = dict(by_id)
173:    for pk in ordered:
174:        pos = pk.stub_pos if pk.player_id is None else pos_of.get(pk.player_id)
175:        if pk.player_id is not None:
176:            pool.pop(pk.player_id, None)
177:        if not pos:
178:            continue
179:        remaining = [_RunPlayer(pos=pos_of.get(pid, ""), adp=_mu_sd(obj)[0]) for pid, obj in pool.items()]
180:        run.observe(pos, remaining=remaining)
181:    return run

184:# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- explanation primitives
185:
186:
187:def _fit_tiers_for_pos(pos_pool: Sequence[BoardPlayer]) -> list[TierInfo]:
188:    """One GMM (or largest-gap) fit per position, NOT per candidate.
189:
190:    A GMM fit is the single most expensive thing this module does (sklearn's k-means init
191:    alone runs ~10-20ms per k searched). ``recommend()`` builds up to
192:    ``n_candidates_per_pos`` candidates at EVERY position, and every one of them needs to know
193:    its own tier -- calling this once per candidate would refit the exact same position's pool
194:    redundantly (4 QB candidates -> the same QB pool fit 4 times). Call it once per position
195:    and hand every candidate at that position the same tier list.
196:    """
197:    if len(pos_pool) >= MIN_N_FOR_GMM:
198:        return TierEngine().update("_", pos_pool)  # fresh engine: no cross-pick hysteresis here
199:    return largest_gap_tiers(pos_pool)
200:
201:
202:def _tier_cliff_from(
203:    tiers: Sequence[TierInfo],
204:    player_id: str,
205:    current_pick: int,
206:    teams: int,
207:    fit: SdFit | None,
208:    run: PositionalRun | None,
209:) -> prim.TierCliff:
210:    for i, t in enumerate(tiers):
211:        ids = {getattr(m, "player_id") for m in t.members}
212:        if player_id in ids:
213:            exhaustion = tier_exhaustion_pick(t.members, current_pick, fit=fit, run=run)
214:            label = snake.pick_label(teams, exhaustion) if exhaustion is not None else None
215:            return prim.TierCliff(
216:                tier_index=i,
217:                tier_size_remaining=t.size,
218:                points_to_next_tier=(t.cliff if t.cliff is not None else 0.0),
219:                exhaustion_pick=exhaustion,
220:                exhaustion_label=label,
221:            )
222:    # Defensive fallback -- every candidate is built from `pos_pool`, so this should not happen.
223:    return prim.TierCliff(tier_index=0, tier_size_remaining=1, points_to_next_tier=0.0, exhaustion_pick=None)
224:
225:
226:def _position_depth(
227:    pos: str,
228:    pos_pool: Sequence[BoardPlayer],
229:    cfg: LeagueConfig,
230:    state: DraftState,
231:    pos_of: Mapping[str, str],
232:    current_pick: int,
233:    fit: SdFit | None,
234:    run: PositionalRun | None,
235:) -> prim.PositionDepth:
236:    startable_remaining = sum(1 for p in pos_pool if p.dv > 0)
237:    league_demand = sum(
238:        state.unfilled_starters(t, cfg.starters, pos_of).get(pos, 0) for t in range(1, state.teams + 1)
239:    )
240:    cushion: float | None = None
241:    if pos_pool:
242:        exhaustion = tier_exhaustion_pick(
243:            pos_pool, current_pick, threshold=max(1.0, float(league_demand)), fit=fit, run=run
244:        )
245:        cushion = float(exhaustion - current_pick) if exhaustion is not None else float(len(pos_pool))
246:    return prim.PositionDepth(
247:        position=pos,
248:        startable_remaining=startable_remaining,
249:        league_demand_remaining=league_demand,
250:        picks_of_cushion=cushion,
251:    )
252:
253:
254:def _opponent_pressure(
255:    pos: str,
256:    ctx,
257:    state: DraftState,
258:    cfg: LeagueConfig,
259:    pos_of: Mapping[str, str],
260:    calibration: opp.LeagueCalibration,
261:    run: PositionalRun | None,
262:) -> prim.OpponentPressure | None:
263:    picks_between = ctx.picks_between_turns
264:    if picks_between is None:
265:        return None
266:
267:    # Count DISTINCT teams, not picks. Two different things: in a snake, the team at the turn picks
268:    # twice in a row, so iterating picks double-counts that manager. It also produced nonsense like
269:    # "16 of the 16 teams" in a 12-team league, which is the kind of visibly-wrong line that makes
270:    # you stop believing the rest of the panel.
271:    slots_before: list[int] = []
272:    for pick in range(state.current_pick + 1, state.current_pick + 1 + picks_between):
273:        slot = snake.slot_on_clock(state.teams, pick)
274:        if slot not in slots_before:
275:            slots_before.append(slot)
276:
277:    teams_before = len(slots_before)
278:    needing = sum(
279:        1
280:        for slot in slots_before
281:        if pos in state.unfilled_starters(slot, cfg.starters, pos_of)
282:    )
283:    offset = calibration.position_timing_offset.get(pos) or None
284:    run_detected = bool(run is not None and run.shift(pos) > 0.0)
285:    return prim.OpponentPressure(
286:        position=pos,
287:        teams_before_next_turn=teams_before,
288:        teams_needing_position=needing,
289:        league_timing_offset=offset,
290:        run_detected=run_detected,
291:    )
292:
293:
294:def _fallbacks(
295:    pos: str,
296:    pos_pool: Sequence[BoardPlayer],
297:    exclude_id: str,
298:    exclude_dv: float,
299:    current_pick: int,
300:    next_pick: int | None,
301:    fit: SdFit | None,
302:    run: PositionalRun | None,
303:    *,
304:    n: int = 2,
305:) -> tuple[prim.Fallback, ...]:
306:    others = sorted((p for p in pos_pool if p.player_id != exclude_id), key=lambda p: -p.dv)[:n]
307:    out: list[prim.Fallback] = []
308:    for alt in others:
309:        if next_pick is None:
310:            p_surv = 1.0
311:        else:
312:            mu, sd = _mu_sd(alt)
313:            if run is not None:
314:                mu = run.adjusted_mu(mu, alt.pos)
315:            p_surv = p_available(mu, sd, next_pick, current_pick, fit=fit)
316:        out.append(
317:            prim.Fallback(
318:                player_id=alt.player_id,
319:                name=alt.name,
320:                pos=pos,
321:                points_behind=exclude_dv - alt.dv,
322:                p_survive_next=p_surv,
323:            )
324:        )
325:    return tuple(out)
326:
327:
328:def _survival_info(
329:    X: BoardPlayer,
330:    ctx,
331:    state: DraftState,
332:    cfg: LeagueConfig,
333:    fit: SdFit | None,
334:    run: PositionalRun | None,
335:) -> prim.SurvivalInfo:
336:    teams = cfg.teams
337:    if ctx.following_pick is None:
338:        # No further turn to worry about -- he's being drafted right now.
339:        return prim.SurvivalInfo(
340:            next_pick=state.current_pick,
341:            next_pick_label=snake.pick_label(teams, state.current_pick),
342:            p_survive_next=1.0,
343:        )
344:    mu, sd = _mu_sd(X)
345:    if run is not None:
346:        mu = run.adjusted_mu(mu, X.pos)
347:    p_next = p_available(mu, sd, ctx.following_pick, state.current_pick, fit=fit)
348:
349:    third_pick = snake.next_pick_for(teams, state.my_slot, state.rounds, ctx.following_pick)
350:    p_following = None
351:    if third_pick is not None:
352:        p_following = p_available(mu, sd, third_pick, state.current_pick, fit=fit)
353:
354:    return prim.SurvivalInfo(
355:        next_pick=ctx.following_pick,
356:        next_pick_label=snake.pick_label(teams, ctx.following_pick),
357:        p_survive_next=p_next,
358:        following_pick=third_pick,
359:        following_pick_label=(snake.pick_label(teams, third_pick) if third_pick is not None else None),
360:        p_survive_following=p_following,
361:    )

363:
364:def _bye_flags(X: BoardPlayer, state: DraftState, by_id: Mapping[str, BoardPlayer]) -> tuple[str, ...]:
365:    if X.bye is None:
366:        return ()
367:    my_ids = {pk.player_id for pk in state.roster(state.my_slot) if pk.player_id is not None}
368:    my_byes = {by_id[pid].bye for pid in my_ids if pid in by_id and by_id[pid].bye is not None}
369:    return ("BYE_COLLISION",) if X.bye in my_byes else ()

371:
372:# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- main entry point
373:
374:
375:def recommend(
376:    state: DraftState,
377:    cfg: LeagueConfig,
378:    players: Sequence[BoardPlayer],
379:    *,
380:    lam: float = DEFAULT_LAM,
381:    calibration: opp.LeagueCalibration | None = None,
382:    n_sims: int = 500,
383:    n_candidates_per_pos: int = 4,
384:    seed: int | None = None,
385:    elite_qb_rank_cutoff: int = ELITE_QB_RANK_CUTOFF,
386:) -> prim.Recommendation:
387:    """Build the full, explained recommendation for whoever is on the clock right now.
388:
389:    Fix "C" (approved 2026-08-17, built 2026-08-18) lives here, in three parts that MOVE THE
390:    RANKING rather than merely append warnings (the old warn-only guardrail was the proven
391:    mechanism behind losing to plain-ADP bots from all 10 slots):
392:
393:    (a) **Reactive scarcity floor.** For any dedicated (non-flex) position where Marc still
394:        has an unfilled starter slot, when `startable supply remaining - leaguewide unfilled
395:        slots <= opponent picks before his next turn`, candidates at that position are ranked
396:        FIRST. Deterministic counting, not a Monte Carlo probability -- the same trigger the
397:        2025 backtest priced at +28.2 points (t=6.6) as `qb_never_below_line`.
398:    (b) **Opportunistic elite grab.** With zero QBs rostered, a top-`elite_qb_rank_cutoff` QB
399:        (by dv, ranked among QBs on the FULL board) still available is ranked first; a QB
400:        below the cutoff is never reached for by this rule. Backtest: +16-18 (t=3.7-4.1).
401:    (c) **Opportunity cost in the ranking.** The off-turn utility adds the position's VONA
402:        (``vona.py``, previously computed and displayed but decision-inert): the Monte Carlo
403:        continuation term is position-agnostic (the best player left at the next turn barely
404:        depends on which candidate was taken), so VONA is exactly the missing positional-decay
405:        term. Fallbacks stay on every candidate, so the tone contract holds: the floor and
406:        the grab re-rank, the human still sees what waiting would have kept.
407:
408:    Args:
409:        state: live draft state (read-only here).
410:        cfg: league rules.
411:        players: the whole player pool, drafted and undrafted, as :class:`BoardPlayer`.
412:        lam: risk-aversion coefficient, ``U = E[value] - lam*SD[value]``. Only used off the
413:            turn; the at-the-turn branch optimizes an exact pair value instead (see module
414:            docstring and CLAUDE.md: "our recommendations never herd", and separately, never
415:            substitute a variance penalty for the exact joint optimum when one is computable).
416:        calibration: opponent calibration; defaults to
417:            :meth:`~draftroom.draft.opponents.LeagueCalibration.national_only`.
418:        n_sims: Monte Carlo trials for the shared roll-forward (see module docstring: one
419:            shared simulation, not one per candidate).
420:        n_candidates_per_pos: how many top-by-draft-value players per position enter the
421:            candidate set before guardrails are applied.
422:        seed: RNG seed for the Monte Carlo roll-forward, for reproducible tests/demos.
423:        elite_qb_rank_cutoff: the fix-"C"(b) knob -- see :data:`ELITE_QB_RANK_CUTOFF`. 0
424:            disables the elite grab.
425:    """
426:    calibration = calibration or opp.LeagueCalibration.national_only()
427:    by_id: dict[str, BoardPlayer] = {p.player_id: p for p in players}
428:    pos_of: dict[str, str] = {p.player_id: p.pos for p in players}
429:    dv_of: dict[str, float] = {p.player_id: p.dv for p in players}
430:
431:    pick_label = snake.pick_label(cfg.teams, state.current_pick)
432:    if not state.is_my_pick:
433:        return prim.Recommendation(
434:            pick_no=state.current_pick,
435:            pick_label=pick_label,
436:            on_the_clock=state.slot_on_clock,
437:            is_my_pick=False,
438:            candidates=(),
439:            warnings=("Not on the clock -- no recommendation generated.",),
440:        )
441:
442:    drafted = state.drafted_player_ids
443:    available = [p for p in players if p.player_id not in drafted]
444:    ctx = state.turn_context()
445:
446:    try:
447:        fit: SdFit | None = fit_sd_model(players)
448:    except ValueError:
449:        fit = None
450:
451:    run = _build_run_detector(state, by_id, pos_of)
452:    warnings: list[str] = []
453:
454:    # ---- candidate generation: top-N by draft value, per position ----
455:    full_pos_pool: dict[str, list[BoardPlayer]] = {
456:        pos: [p for p in available if p.pos == pos] for pos in cfg.positions
457:    }
458:    candidates_by_pos: dict[str, list[BoardPlayer]] = {
459:        pos: sorted(pool, key=lambda p: -p.dv)[:n_candidates_per_pos]
460:        for pos, pool in full_pos_pool.items()
461:    }
462:
463:    # ---- one shared Monte Carlo roll-forward (see module docstring) ----
464:    sims: SimulationSummary | None = None
465:    if ctx.next_pick is not None:
466:        sims = simulate_forward(
467:            state, cfg, players, n_sims=n_sims, calibration=calibration, run_seed=run, seed=seed
468:        )
469:
470:    # ---- guardrail 2: positional shut-out risk, checked for every position with demand ----
471:    if sims is not None and sims.following_pick is not None and sims.results:
472:        for pos in sorted(cfg.positions):
473:            league_demand = sum(
474:                state.unfilled_starters(t, cfg.starters, pos_of).get(pos, 0)
475:                for t in range(1, state.teams + 1)
476:            )
477:            if league_demand <= 0:
478:                continue
479:            hits = 0
480:            for r in sims.results:
481:                if r.survived_at_following is None:
482:                    continue
483:                startable = sum(
484:                    1
485:                    for pid in r.survived_at_following
486:                    if pos_of.get(pid) == pos and dv_of.get(pid, 0.0) > 0
487:                )
488:                if startable < league_demand:
489:                    hits += 1
490:            frac = hits / len(sims.results)
491:            if frac > SHUTOUT_PROB_THRESHOLD:
492:                warnings.append(
493:                    f"CRITICAL: {frac:.0%} chance {pos} startable supply drops below the "
494:                    f"{league_demand} unfilled league slot(s) before your next turn. "
495:                    f"Forcing {pos} into the candidate set."
496:                )
497:                if not candidates_by_pos.get(pos):
498:                    candidates_by_pos[pos] = sorted(full_pos_pool.get(pos, []), key=lambda p: -p.dv)[
499:                        :n_candidates_per_pos
500:                    ]
501:
502:    # ---- fix "C" (a): reactive scarcity floor (deterministic, moves the ranking) ----
503:    my_have = state.roster_positions(state.my_slot, pos_of)
504:    my_unfilled = opp.unfilled_starters_from_counts(my_have, cfg)
505:    gap = ctx.picks_between_turns if ctx.picks_between_turns is not None else 0
506:    floor_positions: set[str] = set()
507:    for pos in sorted(my_unfilled):
508:        if pos in cfg.flex_eligible:
509:            # Flex-eligible supply is fungible across RB/WR/TE, so the per-position count
510:            # below would misfire there; the floor is validated (and needed) for dedicated
511:            # positions -- in this league, QB.
512:            continue
513:        pos_pool_all = full_pos_pool.get(pos, [])
514:        startable_remaining = sum(1 for p in pos_pool_all if p.dv > 0)
515:        league_unfilled = sum(
516:            state.unfilled_starters(t, cfg.starters, pos_of).get(pos, 0)
517:            for t in range(1, state.teams + 1)
518:        )
519:        if startable_remaining - league_unfilled <= gap:
520:            floor_positions.add(pos)
521:            warnings.append(
522:                f"SCARCITY FLOOR: {startable_remaining} startable {pos}s left vs "
523:                f"{league_unfilled} unfilled league slot(s) and {gap} opponent pick(s) before "
524:                f"your next turn -- {pos} ranked first. Fallback shown below."
525:            )
526:            if not candidates_by_pos.get(pos):
527:                candidates_by_pos[pos] = sorted(pos_pool_all, key=lambda p: -p.dv)[
528:                    :n_candidates_per_pos
529:                ]
530:
531:    # ---- fix "C" (b): opportunistic elite QB grab (never reaches below the cutoff) ----
532:    elite_ids: set[str] = set()
533:    if (
534:        elite_qb_rank_cutoff > 0
535:        and cfg.starters.get("QB", 0) > 0
536:        and my_have.get("QB", 0) == 0
537:    ):
538:        qb_rank_full = {
539:            p.player_id: i + 1
540:            for i, p in enumerate(
541:                sorted((q for q in players if q.pos == "QB"), key=lambda p: -p.dv)
542:            )
543:        }
544:        elite_available = [
545:            p
546:            for p in full_pos_pool.get("QB", [])
547:            if qb_rank_full.get(p.player_id, 10**9) <= elite_qb_rank_cutoff
548:        ]
549:        if elite_available:
550:            elite_ids = {p.player_id for p in elite_available}
551:            best_elite = max(elite_available, key=lambda p: p.dv)
552:            warnings.append(
553:                f"ELITE QB AVAILABLE: {best_elite.name} is a top-{elite_qb_rank_cutoff} board "
554:                f"QB and you have 0 of {cfg.starters['QB']} -- ranked first. Fallback shown below."
555:            )
556:            already = {p.player_id for p in candidates_by_pos.get("QB", [])}
557:            for p in elite_available:
558:                if p.player_id not in already:
559:                    candidates_by_pos.setdefault("QB", []).append(p)
560:
561:    flat = [p for pool in candidates_by_pos.values() for p in pool]
562:
563:    # ---- guardrails 1 & 3: exclude infeasible / cap-busting candidates outright ----
564:    feasible = [
565:        p
566:        for p in flat
567:        if _feasible_after_pick(state, cfg, pos_of, p.pos) and _roster_cap_ok(state, cfg, pos_of, p.pos)
568:    ]
569:
570:    vona_map: Mapping[str, VonaResult] = {}
571:    if ctx.following_pick is not None:
572:        vona_map = vona_all_positions(available, state.current_pick, ctx.following_pick, fit=fit, run=run)
573:
574:    is_turn = bool(ctx.at_the_turn and ctx.following_pick is not None)
575:    utilities: dict[str, float] = {}
576:    pair_partner: dict[str, tuple[BoardPlayer | None, float]] = {}
577:
578:    if is_turn:
579:        # Optimise the PAIR jointly: this pick, plus whichever survivor at the very next turn
580:        # maximises DV_X + E[DV_Y], restricted to Y with p_survive >= PAIR_SURVIVAL_FLOOR (spec).
581:        for X in feasible:
582:            best_y: BoardPlayer | None = None
583:            best_p = 0.0
584:            best_val = dv_of[X.player_id]
585:            for Y in available:
586:                if Y.player_id == X.player_id:
587:                    continue
588:                mu, sd = _mu_sd(Y)
589:                if run is not None:
590:                    mu = run.adjusted_mu(mu, Y.pos)
591:                p_surv = p_available(mu, sd, ctx.following_pick, state.current_pick, fit=fit)
592:                if p_surv < PAIR_SURVIVAL_FLOOR:
593:                    continue
594:                val = dv_of[X.player_id] + Y.dv * p_surv
595:                if val > best_val:
596:                    best_val, best_y, best_p = val, Y, p_surv
597:            utilities[X.player_id] = best_val
598:            pair_partner[X.player_id] = (best_y, best_p)
599:        if utilities:
600:            top_id = max(utilities, key=lambda pid: utilities[pid])
601:            y, p = pair_partner.get(top_id, (None, 0.0))
602:            if y is not None:
603:                warnings.append(
604:                    f"AT THE TURN -- best pair: {by_id[top_id].name} now, then {y.name} "
605:                    f"({p:.0%} survives) at {snake.pick_label(cfg.teams, ctx.following_pick)}."
606:                )
607:    else:
608:        # Mid-round: rank by U = E[value] - lam*SD[value]. SD combines two independent sources
609:        # of spread, added in quadrature: X's OWN outcome uncertainty (`dv_sd` -- bust risk /
610:        # boom-bust range on this specific player, a high-ceiling boom-bust WR versus a
611:        # high-floor safe one) and the CONTINUATION uncertainty (what the board looks like at
612:        # Marc's next turn, from the shared Monte Carlo roll-forward). Both read off the same
613:        # shared simulation (see module docstring for why it's shared rather than per-candidate).
614:        for X in feasible:
615:            if sims is not None and sims.following_pick is not None:
616:                dist = sims.best_value_distribution(dv_of, at="following", exclude=X.player_id)
617:                e_val = dv_of[X.player_id] + float(np.mean(dist))
618:                continuation_sd = float(np.std(dist))
619:            else:
620:                e_val = dv_of[X.player_id]
621:                continuation_sd = 0.0
622:            sd_val = float(np.sqrt(X.dv_sd**2 + continuation_sd**2))
623:            # Fix "C" (c): the continuation term above is position-agnostic (the best player
624:            # on the board at the next turn is nearly the same set whichever candidate is
625:            # taken), so the positional cost of WAITING -- VONA -- is added explicitly.
626:            vona_term = vona_map[X.pos].vona if X.pos in vona_map else 0.0
627:            utilities[X.player_id] = e_val - lam * sd_val + vona_term
628:
629:    # One tier fit per POSITION (not per candidate) -- see `_fit_tiers_for_pos`'s docstring.
630:    tiers_by_pos: dict[str, list[TierInfo]] = {
631:        pos: _fit_tiers_for_pos(pool) for pos, pool in full_pos_pool.items() if pool
632:    }
633:
634:    candidates: list[prim.Candidate] = []
635:    for X in feasible:
636:        pos_pool = full_pos_pool[X.pos]
637:        tier = _tier_cliff_from(tiers_by_pos[X.pos], X.player_id, state.current_pick, cfg.teams, fit, run)
638:        survival_info = _survival_info(X, ctx, state, cfg, fit, run)
639:        depth = _position_depth(X.pos, pos_pool, cfg, state, pos_of, state.current_pick, fit, run)
640:        pressure = _opponent_pressure(X.pos, ctx, state, cfg, pos_of, calibration, run)
641:        vona_val = vona_map[X.pos].vona if X.pos in vona_map else 0.0
642:
643:        alt_candidates = [c for c in flat if c.pos != X.pos]
644:        counterfactual = None
645:        if alt_candidates and vona_map:
646:            z = max(alt_candidates, key=lambda p: p.dv)
647:            if z.pos in vona_map and X.pos in vona_map:
648:                counterfactual = prim.Counterfactual(
649:                    position_given_up=z.pos,
650:                    points_given_up=vona_map[z.pos].vona,
651:                    position_gained=X.pos,
652:                    points_gained=vona_map[X.pos].vona,
653:                )
654:
655:        fallbacks = _fallbacks(
656:            X.pos,
657:            pos_pool,
658:            X.player_id,
659:            X.dv,
660:            state.current_pick,
661:            ctx.following_pick,
662:            fit,
663:            run,
664:        )
665:        flags = _bye_flags(X, state, by_id)
666:
667:        candidates.append(
668:            prim.Candidate(
669:                player_id=X.player_id,
670:                name=X.name,
671:                pos=X.pos,
672:                team=X.team,
673:                bye=X.bye,
674:                draft_value=X.dv,
675:                projected_points=X.dv,  # SYNTHETIC proxy until real per-player projections land
676:                floor=X.floor,
677:                ceiling=X.ceiling,
678:                utility=utilities.get(X.player_id, X.dv),
679:                tier=tier,
680:                survival=survival_info,
681:                depth=depth,
682:                vona=vona_val,
683:                opponent_pressure=pressure,
684:                counterfactual=counterfactual,
685:                fallbacks=fallbacks,
686:                flags=flags,
687:            )
688:        )
689:
690:    # Fix "C" ranking: the scarcity floor outranks everything (it is the catastrophe
691:    # avoider, +28.2 in the backtest), the elite grab outranks ordinary value (+16-18),
692:    # utility settles everything else -- including which player leads a forced position.
693:    def _priority(c: prim.Candidate) -> int:
694:        if c.pos in floor_positions:
695:            return 2
696:        if c.player_id in elite_ids:
697:            return 1
698:        return 0
699:
700:    candidates.sort(key=lambda c: (-_priority(c), -c.utility))
701:
702:    rec = prim.Recommendation(
703:        pick_no=state.current_pick,
704:        pick_label=pick_label,
705:        on_the_clock=state.slot_on_clock,
706:        is_my_pick=True,
707:        candidates=tuple(candidates),
708:        warnings=tuple(warnings),
709:        at_the_turn=is_turn,
710:        picks_until_next=ctx.picks_between_turns,
711:    )
712:    return render(rec)

codex
The snake primitives themselves are internally consistent. I’m now checking whether callers use “next pick,” “following pick,” and “opponent picks between turns” consistently; the first material regression I’ve confirmed is that the widened 980-player pool is passed wholesale into the recommender, so zero-value unranked players can become recommendations after the ranked tail is depleted.

1:"""Build a REAL (not synthetic) draft board from cached prep data, for validation tooling.
2:
3:Every other tool in this repo that needs a board either uses hand-built synthetic fixtures
4:(tests) or derives a SYNTHETIC draft value straight from ADP (``tools/demo_recommendation.py``)
5:because a real projections -> valuation pipeline isn't wired end to end yet. This module closes
6:that gap for validation purposes only: it joins the cached Sleeper season projections onto the
7:cached FFC ADP board via the real crosswalk (:mod:`draftroom.prep.crosswalk`), scores each
8:player's projected stat line with the league's OWN scoring PLUS the validated per-game yardage
9:bonus model (:func:`draftroom.prep.scoring.score_statline_with_bonus`, additive on top of the
10:untouched ``score_statline`` dot product -- see that function's docstring; MAE 1.335, mean bias
11:0.254 against the 2025 backtest), and runs the result through the real replacement/EVoB
12:pipeline (:mod:`draftroom.valuation.replacement`, :mod:`draftroom.valuation.evob`). This is the
13:first (and, as of 2026-08-18, only) production path that actually calls the bonus model --
14:``score_statline`` itself stays a pure dot product, untouched, exactly as its docstring
15:requires.
16:
17:Nothing here invents a number: PPG comes from Sleeper's projected stat line divided by Sleeper's
18:own projected games played (passed to :class:`~draftroom.valuation.replacement.PlayerSeason` as
19:an explicit per-player ``expected_games`` override, in preference to the flat positional prior),
20:and ADP/stdev come straight from the cached FFC payload. The only approximation is the join
21:itself (name/ID crosswalk) and the well-documented FFC-is-published-at-12-teams caveat
22:(CLAUDE.md: "One deliberate 12-team exception").
23:
24:Reads only cached files under ``data/raw/`` (:func:`draftroom.prep.http.load_latest_raw`) -- no
25:network call, so this is safe to run offline and on draft night's own machine.

53:__all__ = ["RealBoard", "build_real_board", "SEASON"]

55:log = logging.getLogger("draftroom.validate.board")

57:#: Hardcoded per this repo's convention (prep/fantasypros_client.py, prep/fetch_all.py do the

62:@dataclass(frozen=True)
63:class RealBoard:
64:    """The joined, valued board plus a record of what did NOT make it on, for transparency.
65:
66:    Both ``players`` and ``seasons`` describe the same underlying pool: ``seasons`` is the raw
67:    valuation INPUT (ppg + expected_games, what :func:`~draftroom.valuation.evob.compute_draft_values`
68:    itself takes), ``players`` is the OUTPUT joined onto ADP (what the draft engine's
69:    :class:`~draftroom.draft.recommend.BoardPlayer` needs). Callers doing their own valuation
70:    sweep (e.g. the sanity-invariant gate re-deriving a baseline at a different config) want
71:    ``seasons``; callers driving a mock draft want ``players``.
72:    """
73:
74:    players: tuple[BoardPlayer, ...]
75:    seasons: tuple[PlayerSeason, ...]
76:    #: FFC rows at a skill position that never got a real dv (unresolved, or resolved but with
77:    #: no/zero-game projection). Never silently dropped -- callers can inspect this.
78:    excluded: tuple[AdpRow, ...]
79:    cfg: LeagueConfig
80:    #: Cross-source (Sleeper/FantasyPros/ESPN) projection spread, keyed by player_id. Populated
81:    #: for whichever players resolved onto at least one of those sources -- a player absent
82:    #: here simply had no data to compare (never a fabricated zero). See
83:    #: :mod:`draftroom.valuation.disagreement` and ``disagreement_caveat`` below before reading
84:    #: any of these numbers as a confidence signal.
85:    disagreement: Mapping[str, SourceDisagreement]
86:    #: The mandated caveat (verbatim, see draftroom.valuation.disagreement.DISAGREEMENT_CAVEAT):
87:    #: attached directly to the data, not just to a docstring, so nothing that carries a
88:    #: RealBoard around loses it.
89:    disagreement_caveat: str = DISAGREEMENT_CAVEAT
