"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command 'cmd /c "findstr /n ''^''^ backend'"\\draftroom\\valuation\\evob.py & findstr /n "'^''^ backend'"\\draftroom\\valuation\\disagreement.py\"" in C:\dev\draftroom
 succeeded in 2255ms:
1:"""EVoB -- expected value over baseline, the per-game valuation this model drafts on.
2:
3:``EVoB = (PPG - baseline_PPG) * expected_games``
4:
5:The alternative (season-total VBD, "projected points minus the baseline's projected points")
6:gets the ordering wrong whenever games played differ, which is most of the time. Harstad's
7:example, kept as the first test in the suite: a player who scores 83.2 points in 7 games is
8:worth more than one who scores 84.2 in 16, because in the ten weeks he plays he is beating
9:replacement by six points a game, and the weeks he misses are covered by a bench player who is
10:roughly replacement anyway. Season totals rank them backwards.
11:
12::class:`DraftValue` keeps the components rather than collapsing to one number, because the UI
13:has to explain a pick in two or three bullets each backed by a computed number (CLAUDE.md).
14:"""
15:
16:from __future__ import annotations
17:
18:from dataclasses import dataclass
19:from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping
20:
21:from draftroom.config import LeagueConfig
22:from draftroom.valuation.replacement import (
23:    EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE,
24:    AvailabilityBin,
25:    ReplacementInfo,
26:    replacement_levels,
27:    resolve_players,
28:)
29:
30:__all__ = ["DraftValue", "evob", "compute_draft_values"]
31:
32:
33:def evob(ppg: float, baseline_ppg: float, expected_games: float) -> float:
34:    """Expected points above replacement over the season the player is expected to play."""
35:    return (float(ppg) - float(baseline_ppg)) * float(expected_games)
36:
37:
38:@dataclass(frozen=True)
39:class DraftValue:
40:    """One player's draft value with every input kept visible.
41:
42:    ``dv = evob - lam * sigma_season`` is the risk-adjusted number. ``lam`` is a *preference*,
43:    not an estimate: at 0 the model is risk-neutral and ``dv == evob``.
44:    """
45:
46:    player_id: str
47:    pos: str
48:    ppg: float
49:    baseline_ppg: float
50:    expected_games: float
51:    evob: float
52:    sigma_season: float
53:    lam: float
54:    dv: float
55:    name: str = ""
56:    #: How ``sigma_season`` was obtained -- "given", "from_sigma_ppg", or "absent".
57:    sigma_source: str = "absent"
58:
59:    @property
60:    def points_above_baseline_per_game(self) -> float:
61:        return self.ppg - self.baseline_ppg
62:
63:
64:def _sigma_season_for(player: Any, games: float) -> tuple[float, str]:
65:    """Resolve a player's season-total sigma.
66:
67:    UNVERIFIED MODELING ASSUMPTION: when only a PPG sigma is available, season sigma is taken
68:    as ``sigma_ppg * expected_games`` (perfectly correlated weeks) rather than
69:    ``sigma_ppg * sqrt(expected_games)`` (independent weeks). The dominant uncertainty in a
70:    season projection is talent/role/usage, which persists week to week, so the linear form is
71:    the conservative one -- but this has not been fit to data. Supplying ``sigma_season``
72:    directly bypasses it entirely, and the default of 0.0 means the risk penalty stays inert
73:    until someone deliberately turns it on.
74:    """
75:    if getattr(player, "sigma_season", None) is not None:
76:        return float(player.sigma_season), "given"
77:    sigma_ppg = getattr(player, "sigma_ppg", None)
78:    if sigma_ppg is not None:
79:        return float(sigma_ppg) * float(games), "from_sigma_ppg"
80:    return 0.0, "absent"
81:
82:
83:def compute_draft_values(
84:    players: Iterable[Any],
85:    cfg: LeagueConfig,
86:    lam: float = 0.0,
87:    *,
88:    curves: Mapping[str, tuple[AvailabilityBin, ...]] = EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE,
89:    replacement: Mapping[str, ReplacementInfo] | None = None,
90:) -> dict[str, DraftValue]:
91:    """Draft value for every player, against replacement levels derived from ``cfg``.
92:
93:    Args:
94:        players: player-ish records (see
95:            :func:`~draftroom.valuation.replacement.resolve_players`).
96:        cfg: the league. Every baseline comes from it; nothing is hardcoded.
97:        lam: risk aversion. ``dv = evob - lam * sigma_season``. 0 = risk-neutral.
98:        curves: rank-conditional expected-games curves, see
99:            :data:`~draftroom.valuation.replacement.EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`.
100:        replacement: precomputed baselines, to avoid recomputing during a live draft when the
101:            pool has not changed. Computed from ``players`` when omitted.
102:
103:    Raises:
104:        KeyError: a player sits at a position with no replacement level -- meaning the league
105:            has no lineup demand for it. Scoring such a player against an invented baseline
106:            would be a silently wrong number, so it raises instead.
107:    """
108:    resolved = resolve_players(players, cfg, curves=curves)
109:    levels = (
110:        replacement_levels(resolved, cfg, curves=curves) if replacement is None else replacement
111:    )
112:
113:    out: dict[str, DraftValue] = {}
114:    for p in resolved:
115:        info = levels.get(p.pos)
116:        if info is None:
117:            raise KeyError(
118:                f"player {p.player_id!r} is at position {p.pos!r}, which has no replacement "
119:                f"level in this league (positions with lineup demand: {sorted(levels)}). "
120:                f"Filter the pool to rostered positions before valuing it."
121:            )
122:        games = float(p.expected_games or 0.0)
123:        sigma_season, sigma_source = _sigma_season_for(p, games)
124:        value = evob(p.ppg, info.baseline_ppg, games)
125:        out[p.player_id] = DraftValue(
126:            player_id=p.player_id,
127:            pos=p.pos,
128:            ppg=p.ppg,
129:            baseline_ppg=info.baseline_ppg,
130:            expected_games=games,
131:            evob=value,
132:            sigma_season=sigma_season,
133:            lam=float(lam),
134:            dv=value - float(lam) * sigma_season,
135:            name=p.name,
136:            sigma_source=sigma_source,
137:        )
138:    return out
1:"""Cross-source projection disagreement -- Marc's own idea, approved 2026-08-18, unbuilt until
2:this session.
3:
4:At snapshot/composite time, compute per-player spread across the three INDEPENDENT source
5:families this pipeline has: **Sleeper, FantasyPros (the manual CSVs), and ESPN**. CLAUDE.md is
6:explicit that ESPN's API and the Mike Clay PDF are the SAME source (verified 411/411 identical
7:field-for-field) -- never resolve both into a disagreement measure, or Clay agreeing with
8:himself would make disagreement look artificially small.
9:
10:Two flavours, both computed here:
11:  (a) POINTS spread -- each source's projected stat line scored under the league's own scoring
12:      (:func:`draftroom.prep.scoring.score_statline`), then the spread of those season-total
13:      point figures across whichever source families actually have data for the player.
14:  (b) COMPONENT spread -- the same idea, but broken into passing/rushing/receiving/fumbles
15:      groups, because two sources can agree on a running back's total while disagreeing sharply
16:      on carries vs. catches -- different players entirely in a half-PPR league, and a spread
17:      computed only on the final total would hide exactly that disagreement.
18:
19:THE MANDATED CAVEAT (attach wherever this measure is surfaced -- data, docstrings, UI copy):
20:with only three notionally-independent families, LOW disagreement is not evidence of an
21:accurate projection -- all three can share the same beat-reporter depth chart, the same
22:offseason narrative, and be wrong together. HIGH disagreement is the real signal here; its
23:absence is not a safety signal and must never be read as one.
24:"""
25:
26:from __future__ import annotations
27:
28:from dataclasses import dataclass, field
29:from typing import Mapping, Sequence
30:
31:from draftroom.prep.scoring import score_statline
32:
33:__all__ = [
34:    "INDEPENDENT_SOURCES",
35:    "STAT_GROUPS",
36:    "DISAGREEMENT_CAVEAT",
37:    "SourceDisagreement",
38:    "compute_disagreement",
39:    "sigma_ppg_from_disagreement",
40:]
41:
42:#: The three source families CLAUDE.md confirms are independent. ESPN and the Mike Clay PDF
43:#: are the SAME source and must never both appear as separate entries feeding this measure.
44:INDEPENDENT_SOURCES: tuple[str, ...] = ("sleeper", "fantasypros", "espn")
45:
46:#: Canonical stats grouped by play type, for the component-spread flavour. A stat absent from
47:#: the league's scoring dict simply contributes 0 to its group -- no special-casing needed.
48:STAT_GROUPS: Mapping[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
49:    "passing": ("pass_att", "pass_cmp", "pass_yd", "pass_td", "pass_int", "pass_2pt"),
50:    "rushing": ("rush_att", "rush_yd", "rush_td", "rush_2pt"),
51:    "receiving": ("rec", "rec_tgt", "rec_yd", "rec_td", "rec_2pt"),
52:    "fumbles": ("fum_lost",),
53:}
54:
55:DISAGREEMENT_CAVEAT = (
56:    "Three notionally independent source families feed this measure (Sleeper, FantasyPros, "
57:    "ESPN) -- but they are not three independent looks at reality: all three can lean on the "
58:    "same beat-reporter depth chart or the same offseason narrative and be wrong in the same "
59:    "direction together. HIGH disagreement across them is a real danger signal. LOW "
60:    "disagreement is NOT a safety signal and must never be read as 'this projection is "
61:    "accurate' -- it may just mean all three sources made the same assumption. (CLAUDE.md: "
62:    "ESPN's API and the Mike Clay PDF are ONE source, verified 411/411 identical -- never "
63:    "counted as two of the three families here.)"
64:)
65:
66:
67:@dataclass(frozen=True)
68:class SourceDisagreement:
69:    """One player's cross-source spread, both flavours, with every input kept visible."""
70:
71:    player_id: str
72:    #: Which of INDEPENDENT_SOURCES actually had a statline for this player, sorted.
73:    sources_present: tuple[str, ...]
74:    #: source -> season-total fantasy points under the league's own scoring.
75:    points_by_source: Mapping[str, float]
76:    points_mean: float
77:    #: Population stdev of points_by_source's values. 0.0 (not missing) when < 2 sources.
78:    points_stdev: float
79:    #: max - min across points_by_source's values. 0.0 when < 2 sources.
80:    points_range: float
81:    #: group name (see STAT_GROUPS) -> population stdev of that group's points across sources.
82:    component_stdev: Mapping[str, float]
83:    n_sources: int
84:
85:    @property
86:    def has_disagreement_signal(self) -> bool:
87:        """False when fewer than 2 independent sources had data -- there is nothing to
88:        compare, and points_stdev/component_stdev being 0.0 in that case must not be read as
89:        'sources agree.' Callers should check this before trusting a 0.0 spread."""
90:        return self.n_sources >= 2
91:
92:
93:def _population_stdev(values: Sequence[float]) -> float:
94:    n = len(values)
95:    if n < 2:
96:        return 0.0
97:    mean = sum(values) / n
98:    variance = sum((v - mean) ** 2 for v in values) / n
99:    return variance**0.5
100:
101:
102:def compute_disagreement(
103:    player_id: str,
104:    stats_by_source: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, float]],
105:    scoring: Mapping[str, float],
106:) -> SourceDisagreement:
107:    """Build one player's :class:`SourceDisagreement` from whichever source statlines exist.
108:
109:    Args:
110:        player_id: the resolved (crosswalk) player id these statlines share.
111:        stats_by_source: source name -> canonical stat mapping (e.g. ``StatLine.as_dict()``).
112:            Only keys in :data:`INDEPENDENT_SOURCES` are considered; a source absent from this
113:            mapping (or explicitly ``None``) simply does not contribute -- never fabricated.
114:        scoring: the league's own scoring (``LeagueConfig.scoring``).
115:    """
116:    sources_present = tuple(
117:        sorted(s for s in INDEPENDENT_SOURCES if stats_by_source.get(s) is not None)
118:    )
119:    points_by_source = {s: score_statline(stats_by_source[s], scoring) for s in sources_present}
120:    values = list(points_by_source.values())
121:    n = len(values)
122:    points_mean = sum(values) / n if n else 0.0
123:    points_stdev = _population_stdev(values)
124:    points_range = (max(values) - min(values)) if n >= 2 else 0.0
125:
126:    component_stdev: dict[str, float] = {}
127:    for group, stat_names in STAT_GROUPS.items():
128:        group_scoring = {k: v for k, v in scoring.items() if k in stat_names}
129:        if not group_scoring:
130:            continue  # league doesn't score anything in this group -- nothing to disagree on
131:        group_values = [score_statline(stats_by_source[s], group_scoring) for s in sources_present]
132:        component_stdev[group] = _population_stdev(group_values)
133:
134:    return SourceDisagreement(
135:        player_id=player_id,
136:        sources_present=sources_present,
137:        points_by_source=points_by_source,
138:        points_mean=points_mean,
139:        points_stdev=points_stdev,
140:        points_range=points_range,
141:        component_stdev=component_stdev,
142:        n_sources=n,
143:    )
144:
145:
146:def sigma_ppg_from_disagreement(d: SourceDisagreement, expected_games: float) -> float | None:
147:    """Map the points-spread into a PPG sigma :class:`~draftroom.valuation.evob.DraftValue` can
148:    consume, so ``sigma_source`` stops reading "absent" for players with real cross-source data.
149:
150:    UNVERIFIED MODELING ASSUMPTION, documented once here rather than at each call site: this
151:    divides the season-POINTS stdev by ``expected_games`` to land on a PPG-scale figure. Each
152:    source may implicitly assume a different games count (Sleeper and ESPN both publish their
153:    own per-player figure; FantasyPros publishes none at all -- see prep/manual_csv.py's
154:    2026-08-18 fix), so part of what this measures is disagreement about DURABILITY, not just
155:    about weekly rate. That conflation is a known limitation, not a hidden one.
156:
157:    Returns ``None`` (never a fabricated 0.0) when there are fewer than 2 independent sources
158:    or no games to divide by -- see :attr:`SourceDisagreement.has_disagreement_signal`.
159:
160:    CAVEAT (see module-level ``DISAGREEMENT_CAVEAT``, same wording, attached here again because
161:    a sigma value on its own is exactly the kind of number someone reads out of context): a low
162:    sigma here is NOT evidence the projection is safe -- with only three correlated source
163:    families, it may just mean they all made the same assumption. High disagreement is the real
164:    signal; its absence is not a safety signal.
165:    """
166:    if not d.has_disagreement_signal or expected_games <= 0:
167:        return None
168:    return d.points_stdev / float(expected_games)

"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command 'cmd /c findstr /n ''^''^ backend'"\\draftroom\\draft\\vona.py" in C:\dev\draftroom
 succeeded in 3076ms:
1:"""VONA -- value of next available: the live cost of waiting one turn.
2:
3:"Can I afford to wait on this position?" is the single most common question at the table, and
4:the honest answer needs an *expectation*, not the naive "take the ADP-best guy who'll be there
5:next time". :func:`expected_best_available` is an order-statistic expectation over the
6:remaining pool: the expected draft value of whichever player turns out to be the best one still
7:standing at a future pick, given every player's own conditional survival probability
8:(:mod:`draftroom.draft.survival`).
9:
10::func:`vona` is then just today's best at a position minus that expectation one turn later. A
11:position with a hard cliff (one great player, then a canyon) has high VONA -- waiting is
12:expensive because the expectation collapses once that one player is gone. A deep position (WR
13:in most leagues) has low VONA -- the expected best-available barely moves, because there is
14:always another very-similar player behind the one you'd take now.
15:"""
16:
17:from __future__ import annotations
18:
19:from dataclasses import dataclass
20:from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence
21:
22:from draftroom.draft.survival import PositionalRun, SdFit, _mu_sd, _pos_of, p_available
23:
24:__all__ = ["VonaResult", "expected_best_available", "vona", "vona_all_positions"]
25:
26:
27:def _dv_of(player: Any) -> float:
28:    """Pull a scalar draft value out of anything player-ish (mirrors ``tiers.dynamic``)."""
29:    if isinstance(player, Mapping):
30:        raw = player.get("dv", player.get("draft_value"))
31:    else:
32:        raw = getattr(player, "dv", None)
33:        if raw is None:
34:            raw = getattr(player, "draft_value", None)
35:    if raw is None:
36:        raise KeyError(f"no draft-value field (dv/draft_value) on {player!r}")
37:    return float(raw)
38:
39:
40:def expected_best_available(
41:    players: Sequence[Any],
42:    target_pick: float,
43:    current_pick: float,
44:    *,
45:    fit: SdFit | None = None,
46:    run: PositionalRun | None = None,
47:) -> float:
48:    """``E[max draft value among survivors at target_pick]``.
49:
50:    An order-statistic expectation, built from each player's own conditional survival
51:    probability (:func:`~draftroom.draft.survival.p_available`): sort by draft value
52:    descending, then the "best-available" event for player ``i`` is "``i`` survives to
53:    ``target_pick`` AND every player with strictly higher draft value does not". Treating
54:    survival as independent across players (the same approximation
55:    :func:`~draftroom.draft.survival.expected_survivors` makes, and for the same reason: only
56:    the spread of the estimate is affected, not its mean) turns that into a running product:
57:
58:    ``E = sum_i DV_i * P(i alive) * prod_{j better than i} (1 - P(j alive))``
59:
60:    The missing probability mass (every ranked player already gone) contributes an implicit
61:    zero -- "best available" is then whatever replacement-level flex fill-in the roster
62:    algorithm would find, which is out of scope for this function.
63:    """
64:    ranked = sorted(players, key=lambda p: -_dv_of(p))
65:    total = 0.0
66:    prob_all_better_gone = 1.0
67:    for player in ranked:
68:        mu, sd = _mu_sd(player)
69:        if run is not None:
70:            mu = run.adjusted_mu(mu, _pos_of(player))
71:        p_alive = p_available(mu, sd, target_pick, current_pick, fit=fit)
72:        total += _dv_of(player) * p_alive * prob_all_better_gone
73:        prob_all_better_gone *= 1.0 - p_alive
74:    return total
75:
76:
77:@dataclass(frozen=True)
78:class VonaResult:
79:    """One position's live wait cost, with the two numbers that produced it kept visible."""
80:
81:    position: str
82:    best_now: float
83:    expected_next: float
84:
85:    @property
86:    def vona(self) -> float:
87:        return self.best_now - self.expected_next
88:
89:    def describe(self) -> str:
90:        return (
91:            f"{self.position}: best now {self.best_now:.1f}, expected next turn "
92:            f"{self.expected_next:.1f} -> VONA {self.vona:.1f}"
93:        )
94:
95:
96:def vona(
97:    position: str,
98:    players: Sequence[Any],
99:    current_pick: float,
100:    next_pick: float,
101:    *,
102:    fit: SdFit | None = None,
103:    run: PositionalRun | None = None,
104:) -> VonaResult:
105:    """``DV(best available now at pos) - E[DV(best available at pos at next_pick)]``.
106:
107:    ``players`` should be the currently-remaining pool (any positions; this filters). The
108:    "best available now" term needs no survival math at all -- the pool given IS who is on the
109:    board right now, so it is simply the max draft value in it.
110:    """
111:    pos = str(position).upper()
112:    pool = [p for p in players if _pos_of(p) == pos]
113:    if not pool:
114:        return VonaResult(position=pos, best_now=0.0, expected_next=0.0)
115:
116:    best_now = max(_dv_of(p) for p in pool)
117:    expected_next = expected_best_available(pool, next_pick, current_pick, fit=fit, run=run)
118:    return VonaResult(position=pos, best_now=best_now, expected_next=expected_next)
119:
120:
121:def vona_all_positions(
122:    players: Sequence[Any],
123:    current_pick: float,
124:    next_pick: float,
125:    *,
126:    fit: SdFit | None = None,
127:    run: PositionalRun | None = None,
128:) -> dict[str, VonaResult]:
129:    """:func:`vona` for every position present in ``players`` -- the UI's cheat-sheet row."""
130:    positions = sorted({_pos_of(p) for p in players})
131:    return {
132:        pos: vona(pos, players, current_pick, next_pick, fit=fit, run=run) for pos in positions
133:    }

66:
67:def test_qb_startable_rank_scales_with_demand():
68:    """Updated 2026-08-18 for the rank-conditional availability curve: doubling the man-games
69:    demand must deepen the startable rank by MORE than double, not exactly double.
70:
71:    "Exactly double" was only true under the old FLAT per-position prior (every QB rank
72:    contributed the same ~15.6 games, so man-games supply was linear in rank and doubling
73:    demand doubled the crossing rank arithmetically). The real, fitted curve's games-per-rank
74:    shrinks fast past the top of the position (QB rank 25-40 averages far fewer games than
75:    rank 1-15 -- see replacement.py's EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE), so each additional rank past the
76:    original crossing supplies fewer man-games than the ranks before it. Reaching double the
77:    demand therefore needs MORE than double the ranks -- 96, not 44, at 20 teams -- which is
78:    exactly the shape the games-played fix exists to capture, not a bug in this test.
79:    """
80:    cfg10 = make_cfg()
81:    cfg20 = make_cfg(teams=20)
82:    rank_10 = st.qb_startable_rank(cfg10)
83:    rank_20 = st.qb_startable_rank(cfg20)
84:    assert rank_10 == 22
85:    assert rank_20 == 96
86:    assert rank_20 > 2 * rank_10, (
87:        f"a realistic (non-flat) availability curve must need MORE than double the rank to "
88:        f"cover double the demand: {rank_10} -> {rank_20}"
89:    )
90:    # Fewer weeks -> less demand AND smaller per-rank availability (both scale by weeks/17,
91:    # uniformly across every rank) -> the crossing rank itself is unchanged.
92:    cfg2 = make_cfg(weeks=8)
93:    assert st.qb_startable_rank(cfg2) == st.qb_startable_rank(make_cfg())

131:    )
132:    assert chosen == "rb1"  # higher dv wins -- deadline hasn't bound yet

150:def test_qb_need_already_met_ignores_deadline_and_reactive_logic():
151:    """Once both starting QB slots are filled, EVERY strategy (fixed-round or reactive)
152:    collapses to pure best-value -- this is the guarantee that a strategy never drafts a 3rd/4th
153:    QB out of leftover positional logic."""
154:    cfg = make_cfg()
155:    available = [player("qb1", "QB", 500.0), player("rb1", "RB", 10.0)]
156:    have = _have_all(cfg, my_slot=1, my_qb=2)  # need already met
157:    for strat in (st.Strategy("qb_elite", 4), st.Strategy("qb_never_below_line", None, reactive=True)):
158:        chosen = st.strategy_pick(
159:            strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=1, cfg=cfg,
160:            qb_rank={"qb1": 1}, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
161:        )
162:        assert chosen == "qb1"  # best value overall, not "avoid QB because need is met"

165:def test_best_value_never_forces_qb_regardless_of_scarcity_or_round():
166:    """The control: qb_deadline_round=None, reactive=False must NEVER force a QB, even in the
167:    most extreme scarcity (every other team has already used all its QB slots, one startable QB
168:    left, my pick is right before a long gap) -- the only lever that exists is dv."""
169:    cfg = make_cfg()
170:    strat = st.Strategy("best_value", None, reactive=False)
171:    have = {t: {"QB": 2} for t in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
172:    have[1] = {"QB": 0}  # I'm the only one who still needs QBs, and only 1 remains
173:    available = [player("qb1", "QB", 400.0), player("wr1", "WR", 10.0)]
174:    chosen = st.strategy_pick(
175:        strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=1, cfg=cfg,
176:        qb_rank={"qb1": 1}, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
177:    )
178:    assert chosen == "qb1"  # only because it also has the highest dv here
179:    # Now flip the dv so the QB is NOT the best value -- best_value must still skip it.
180:    available2 = [player("qb1", "QB", 5.0), player("wr1", "WR", 400.0)]
181:    chosen2 = st.strategy_pick(
182:        strat, available2, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=1, cfg=cfg,
183:        qb_rank={"qb1": 1}, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
184:    )
185:    assert chosen2 == "wr1"

188:def test_reactive_trigger_fires_when_startable_supply_meets_leaguewide_demand():
189:    cfg = make_cfg()
190:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_never_below_line", None, reactive=True)
191:    # All 10 teams (including mine) still need both QB slots: leaguewide unfilled = 10*2 = 20.
192:    have = {t: {"QB": 0} for t in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
193:    # Only 18 startable QBs remain -- supply already BELOW demand -- must trigger regardless of gap.
194:    available = [player(f"qb{i}", "QB", 100.0 - i) for i in range(18)] + [player("wr1", "WR", 500.0)]
195:    qb_rank = {p.player_id: i + 1 for i, p in enumerate(available) if p.pos == "QB"}
196:    pick_no = st.snake.overall_pick(cfg.teams, 1, 1)  # round 1, slot 1 -- gap to next turn is large
197:    chosen = st.strategy_pick(
198:        strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
199:        qb_rank=qb_rank, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
200:    )
201:    assert chosen.startswith("qb")  # forced, even though wr1 has far higher dv

204:def test_reactive_trigger_does_not_fire_with_ample_startable_supply():
205:    cfg = make_cfg()
206:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_never_below_line", None, reactive=True)
207:    have = {t: {"QB": 0} for t in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}  # leaguewide unfilled = 10*2 = 20
208:    # Round 1, slot 1: next turn is pick 20 (round 2, snake), so gap = 20 - 1 - 1 = 18.
209:    # 45 startable QBs remain -- comfortably more than unfilled(20) + gap(18) = 38 -- so the
210:    # line is nowhere close and best-value should win.
211:    available = [player(f"qb{i}", "QB", 50.0 - i) for i in range(45)] + [player("wr1", "WR", 500.0)]
212:    qb_rank = {p.player_id: i + 1 for i, p in enumerate(available) if p.pos == "QB"}
213:    pick_no = st.snake.overall_pick(cfg.teams, 1, 1)
214:    chosen = st.strategy_pick(
215:        strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
216:        qb_rank=qb_rank, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=44,
217:    )
218:    assert chosen == "wr1"

221:def test_reactive_trigger_ignores_qbs_below_the_startable_cutoff():
222:    """A remaining QB ranked worse than `qb_startable_rank_cutoff` (a token QB4/QB5 nobody would
223:    ever start) must NOT count as supply -- this is the exact bug caught in dry-run testing
224:    (an earlier version counted every leftover QB regardless of quality and the trigger never
225:    fired)."""
226:    cfg = make_cfg()
227:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_never_below_line", None, reactive=True)
228:    have = {t: {"QB": 0} for t in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
229:    # Only 3 QBs remain in the pool, but their qb_rank (60, 61, 62) is far below the cutoff (22)
230:    # -- none of them count as "startable" supply, so the trigger must still fire.
231:    available = [
232:        player("qb_deep1", "QB", 5.0), player("qb_deep2", "QB", 4.0), player("qb_deep3", "QB", 3.0),
233:        player("wr1", "WR", 500.0),
234:    ]
235:    qb_rank = {"qb_deep1": 60, "qb_deep2": 61, "qb_deep3": 62}
236:    pick_no = st.snake.overall_pick(cfg.teams, 1, 1)
237:    chosen = st.strategy_pick(
238:        strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
239:        qb_rank=qb_rank, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
240:    )
241:    assert chosen.startswith("qb_deep")  # forced despite low dv -- 0 startable QBs is the point

247:def test_elite_one_cheap_deadline_is_round_one_and_stays_forced_in_later_rounds_too():
248:    """ELITE_QB_DEADLINE_ROUND == 1 (see the constant's docstring: elite QBs are gone from the
249:    WHOLE draft, not just this team's board, well before round 4) -- the rule engages on a
250:    strategy's very first pick, and the `>=` comparison means it stays engaged at any later
251:    round too, as long as my_qb is still 0 and an elite QB happens to still be sitting there."""
252:    cfg = make_cfg()
253:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_one_elite_one_cheap", elite_one_cheap=True)
254:    have = _have_all(cfg, my_slot=1, my_qb=0)
255:    available = [player("qb_elite", "QB", 50.0), player("rb1", "RB", 120.0)]
256:    for rnd in (1, 3):
257:        pick_no = st.snake.overall_pick(cfg.teams, rnd, 1)
258:        chosen = st.strategy_pick(
259:            strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
260:            qb_rank={"qb_elite": 1}, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
261:        )
262:        assert chosen == "qb_elite", f"round {rnd}"

265:def test_elite_one_cheap_forces_qb_at_deadline_only_if_elite_tier_present():
266:    cfg = make_cfg()
267:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_one_elite_one_cheap", elite_one_cheap=True)
268:    have = _have_all(cfg, my_slot=1, my_qb=0)
269:    pick_no = st.snake.overall_pick(cfg.teams, st.ELITE_QB_DEADLINE_ROUND, 1)
270:
271:    # Elite tier (rank <= ELITE_QB_RANK_CUTOFF) still on the board -- forced, despite lower dv.
272:    available = [player("qb_elite", "QB", 50.0), player("rb1", "RB", 120.0)]
273:    chosen = st.strategy_pick(
274:        strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
275:        qb_rank={"qb_elite": st.ELITE_QB_RANK_CUTOFF}, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
276:    )

280:def test_elite_one_cheap_does_not_reach_for_a_mediocre_qb_once_elite_tier_is_gone():
281:    """The whole point of the compound rule: it never substitutes a below-elite QB just to hit
282:    the deadline -- if rank 1-3 is gone, it falls through to best value like the control."""
283:    cfg = make_cfg()
284:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_one_elite_one_cheap", elite_one_cheap=True)
285:    have = _have_all(cfg, my_slot=1, my_qb=0)
286:    pick_no = st.snake.overall_pick(cfg.teams, st.ELITE_QB_DEADLINE_ROUND, 1)
287:    available = [player("qb_mediocre", "QB", 20.0), player("rb1", "RB", 50.0)]
288:    chosen = st.strategy_pick(
289:        strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
290:        qb_rank={"qb_mediocre": st.ELITE_QB_RANK_CUTOFF + 5}, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
291:    )
292:    assert chosen == "rb1"  # NOT qb_mediocre -- it would win on raw dv if this rule "reached"

295:def test_elite_one_cheap_second_qb_uses_the_reactive_scarcity_trigger():
296:    """Once the elite QB1 is rostered (my_qb == 1), the second QB slot must behave exactly like
297:    `qb_never_below_line` -- forced only when startable supply meets leaguewide demand."""
298:    cfg = make_cfg()
299:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_one_elite_one_cheap", elite_one_cheap=True)
300:    have = {t: {"QB": 0} for t in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
301:    have[1] = {"QB": 1}  # elite QB1 already rostered
302:    # Ample supply -- 45 startable QBs vs. 19 leaguewide unfilled (9 other teams x2 + my 1) plus
303:    # a large gap at round 1 -- should NOT trigger.
304:    available = [player(f"qb{i}", "QB", 50.0 - i) for i in range(45)] + [player("wr1", "WR", 500.0)]
305:    qb_rank = {p.player_id: i + 1 for i, p in enumerate(available) if p.pos == "QB"}
306:    pick_no = st.snake.overall_pick(cfg.teams, 1, 1)
307:    chosen = st.strategy_pick(
308:        strat, available, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
309:        qb_rank=qb_rank, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=44,
310:    )
311:    assert chosen == "wr1"
312:
313:    # Now starve supply down to right at the line -- must trigger.
314:    available_scarce = [player("qb_last", "QB", 5.0), player("wr1", "WR", 500.0)]
315:    chosen2 = st.strategy_pick(
316:        strat, available_scarce, have, my_slot=1, pick_no=pick_no, cfg=cfg,
317:        qb_rank={"qb_last": 1}, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=22,
318:    )
319:    assert chosen2 == "qb_last"

324:
325:def _small_universe(cfg: LeagueConfig) -> list[BoardPlayer]:
326:    """Enough players to complete a full draft at this (small) league's roster_size, with QB
327:    plentiful enough that a deadline strategy can always find one."""
328:    players: list[BoardPlayer] = []
329:    for i in range(30):
330:        players.append(player(f"qb{i}", "QB", 200.0 - i))
331:    for i in range(60):
332:        players.append(player(f"rb{i}", "RB", 195.0 - i))
333:    for i in range(80):
334:        players.append(player(f"wr{i}", "WR", 190.0 - i))
335:    for i in range(30):
336:        players.append(player(f"te{i}", "TE", 150.0 - i))
337:    return players

340:def test_run_one_draft_fills_every_roster_slot_and_deadline_strategy_completes_qb2_on_time():
341:    cfg = make_cfg()
342:    players = _small_universe(cfg)
343:    draft_players_by_id = {p.player_id: p for p in players}
344:    resolved = {p.player_id: (p.adp, p.stdev, p.pos) for p in players}
345:    qb_rank = st.build_qb_rank(players)
346:    cutoff = st.qb_startable_rank(cfg)
347:
348:    strat = st.Strategy("qb_elite", 4)
349:    rosters = st.run_one_draft(
350:        seed=1, strategy=strat, strategy_slot=3, draft_players_by_id=draft_players_by_id,
351:        resolved=resolved, cfg=cfg, qb_rank=qb_rank, qb_startable_rank_cutoff=cutoff,
352:    )
353:    for slot in range(1, cfg.teams + 1):
354:        assert len(rosters[slot]) == cfg.roster_size
355:
356:    my_ids = rosters[3]
357:    qb_seen = 0
358:    round_of_2nd_qb = None
359:    for rnd, pid in enumerate(my_ids, start=1):
360:        if draft_players_by_id[pid].pos == "QB":
361:            qb_seen += 1
362:            if qb_seen == 2:
363:                round_of_2nd_qb = rnd
364:                break
365:    assert round_of_2nd_qb is not None
366:    assert round_of_2nd_qb <= 4  # qb_elite's deadline

392:def test_starting_lineup_value_reused_unmodified_scores_a_full_roster():
393:    """Smoke test that `mock_draft_sim.starting_lineup_value` (imported, never reimplemented)
394:    works against a `scoring_players_by_id` map built the way `main()` builds it: same
395:    BoardPlayer objects with `dv` swapped for a separate scoring value."""
396:    from dataclasses import replace
397:
398:    cfg = make_cfg()
399:    draft_board = {
400:        "qb1": player("qb1", "QB", 10.0), "qb2": player("qb2", "QB", 5.0),
401:        "rb1": player("rb1", "RB", 10.0), "rb2": player("rb2", "RB", 8.0),
402:        "wr1": player("wr1", "WR", 10.0), "wr2": player("wr2", "WR", 8.0), "wr3": player("wr3", "WR", 6.0),
403:        "te1": player("te1", "TE", 10.0),
404:        "flex_rb": player("flex_rb", "RB", 20.0),
405:    }
406:    scoring_dv = {"qb1": 100.0, "qb2": 50.0, "rb1": 30.0, "rb2": 20.0, "wr1": 40.0, "wr2": 25.0,
407:                  "wr3": 15.0, "te1": 12.0, "flex_rb": 60.0}
408:    scoring_board = {pid: replace(p, dv=scoring_dv[pid]) for pid, p in draft_board.items()}
409:
410:    roster_ids = list(draft_board.keys())
411:    value = st.starting_lineup_value(roster_ids, scoring_board, cfg)
412:    # Optimal lineup: QB x2 (100+50), RB x2 best-of-{rb1,rb2,flex_rb}=flex_rb(60)+rb1(30),
413:    # WR x3 (40+25+15), TE x1 (12), flex takes the next best RB/WR/TE leftover = rb2 (20).
414:    expected = 100 + 50 + 60 + 30 + 40 + 25 + 15 + 12 + 20
415:    assert value == pytest.approx(expected)

"C:\\Windows\\System32\\WindowsPowerShell\\v1.0\\powershell.exe" -Command "cmd /c more +20 tools\\mock_draft_sim.py" in C:\dev\draftroom
 succeeded in 766ms:

OBJECTIVE FUNCTION: sum of real `dv` (season EVoB, points above the league's own real
replacement baseline) across the OPTIMAL starting lineup the final 15-man roster supports --
QB x2, RB x2, WR x3, TE x1, one RB/WR/TE flex (`cfg.starters`/`cfg.flex_*`) -- not the whole
bench. Bench depth that never starts scores zero points in real life, so crediting it would
reward hoarding over the players who actually win weeks. Because starters at each dedicated
position are disjoint and only the single flex slot creates any cross-position choice, filling
each dedicated slot with the position's own top-N-by-dv players and then the flex slot with the
single best leftover flex-eligible player is the OPTIMAL assignment, not a heuristic.

UNFILLED MANDATORY SLOTS ARE CHARGED, NOT FREE (fixed 2026-08-18). The original objective
implicitly scored an unfilled slot at 0 dv -- i.e. handed a roster that drafted ZERO quarterbacks
two replacement-level QBs for free, which is how a no-QB strategy once "won" a tournament at
639.3 while rostering zero QBs in 2000/2000 drafts. Two levers were considered: (a) enforcing
weekly lineup constraints in the sum -- already the case here, bench never scores and a 5th RB
cannot occupy a QB slot; and (b) pricing a truly unfilled slot at what the post-draft waiver
wire actually offers, minus a realism penalty. (b) is implemented by `waiver_fill_values`: an
unfilled slot is credited `min(0, median dv of the top-`cfg.teams` UNDRAFTED players at that
position)`. The median-of-the-contested-band (not the single best leftover) IS the realism
penalty -- ten teams work the same wire, so you do not get first pick of it -- expressed
structurally instead of as a magic constant. The cap at 0 denies hindsight credit for an
undrafted breakout (in historical mode a real breakout can carry hugely positive real dv;
"I'd have picked him up week 1" is exactly the look-ahead this tournament exists to exclude).

PASS BAR (spec): the engine's roster lands at or above the 65th percentile of rosters
achievable from that same draft slot. "Achievable from that slot" is estimated by the
bot-only baseline distribution for that slot -- what a fully ADP/need/herd-following manager
gets, facing nine others just like it, from each of the ten seats. The 2026 draft slot is
unknown until draft night, so every slot 1-10 is run and reported separately; a model that only
clears the bar from slot 1 is not ready (spec).

Run:
    C:\\dev\\draftroom\\.venv\\Scripts\\python.exe tools\\mock_draft_sim.py [--engine-reps N]
        [--baseline-reps N] [--n-sims N] [--seed N]
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import logging
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_ROOT / "backend"))

import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

from draftroom.draft import opponents as opp  # noqa: E402
from draftroom.draft import snake  # noqa: E402
from draftroom.draft.recommend import BoardPlayer, recommend  # noqa: E402
from draftroom.draft.state import DraftState, Pick  # noqa: E402
from draftroom.draft.survival import PositionalRun  # noqa: E402
from draftroom.validate.board import build_real_board  # noqa: E402

PASS_PERCENTILE = 65.0


# ============================================================================== objective


def waiver_fill_values(undrafted_players, cfg) -> dict[str, float]:
    """dv credited to a mandatory lineup slot the drafted roster cannot fill, per position.

    `min(0.0, median dv of the top-cfg.teams undrafted players at the position)`. See the
    module docstring ("UNFILLED MANDATORY SLOTS ARE CHARGED") for why the median of the
    contested band is the waiver proxy and why the cap at 0 exists. A position with no
    undrafted players at all (should not happen -- every board here carries more players than
    the league drafts) falls back to the worst undrafted dv anywhere, still capped at 0.
    """
    undrafted = list(undrafted_players)
    all_dvs = [p.dv for p in undrafted]
    floor_dv = min(0.0, min(all_dvs)) if all_dvs else 0.0
    fill: dict[str, float] = {}
    for pos in set(cfg.starters) | set(cfg.flex_eligible):
        band = sorted((p.dv for p in undrafted if p.pos == pos), reverse=True)[: cfg.teams]
        if band:
            mid = band[(len(band) - 1) // 2]  # upper median: kind to the roster, still contested
            fill[pos] = min(0.0, mid)
        else:
            fill[pos] = floor_dv
    return fill


def starting_lineup_value(player_ids, players_by_id, cfg, *, waiver_fill=None) -> float:
    """Sum of `dv` across the OPTIMAL starting lineup this roster supports. See module
    docstring for why greedy-per-position-then-flex is exactly optimal here, not a heuristic.

    `waiver_fill` (from :func:`waiver_fill_values`) prices any mandatory slot the roster
    cannot fill from its own players. `None` preserves the legacy unfilled-slot-scores-0
    behavior for callers that guarantee full rosters; every scoring path in this tool and in
    `strategy_tournament.py` passes it.
    """
    by_pos: dict[str, list[BoardPlayer]] = {}
    for pid in player_ids:
        p = players_by_id[pid]
        by_pos.setdefault(p.pos, []).append(p)
    for pos in by_pos:
        by_pos[pos].sort(key=lambda p: -p.dv)

    total = 0.0
    used: set[str] = set()
    for pos, need in cfg.starters.items():
        own = by_pos.get(pos, [])[:need]
        for p in own:
            total += p.dv
            used.add(p.player_id)
        if waiver_fill is not None and len(own) < need:
            total += (need - len(own)) * waiver_fill.get(pos, 0.0)

    flex_pool = sorted(
        (p for pos in cfg.flex_eligible for p in by_pos.get(pos, []) if p.player_id not in used),
        key=lambda p: -p.dv,
    )
    n_flex_own = min(cfg.flex_slots, len(flex_pool))
    for p in flex_pool[:n_flex_own]:
        total += p.dv
    if waiver_fill is not None and n_flex_own < cfg.flex_slots:
        best_flex_fill = max(
            (waiver_fill.get(pos, 0.0) for pos in cfg.flex_eligible), default=0.0
        )
        total += (cfg.flex_slots - n_flex_own) * best_flex_fill
    return total


def waiver_fill_for_draft(rosters: dict[int, list[str]], players_by_id, cfg) -> dict[str, float]:
    """The per-position waiver proxy for one FINISHED draft -- computed from what that draft
    actually left undrafted (it differs draft to draft), once, then reused for all 10 rosters."""
    drafted: set[str] = set()
    for ids in rosters.values():
        drafted.update(ids)
    undrafted = [p for pid, p in players_by_id.items() if pid not in drafted]
    return waiver_fill_values(undrafted, cfg)


# ============================================================================== room-2025 bots
#
# A second opponent room ("--room room2025"): bots whose POSITIONAL behavior is sampled from
# the real 2025 draft of this exact league (`data/draft_2025.csv`) -- 6 first-round QBs, the
# rounds-5-6 QB run, every team hoarding 3+ QBs (31 total, max 4 on one roster). The ADP-model
# room answers "does the engine beat a market-priced room"; this one answers "does it beat the
# room Marc actually sits in". Position first (empirical per-round frequencies), then the
# player at that position by the same ADP softmax the standard bot uses -- so the two rooms
# differ ONLY in positional pacing, never in within-position player choice.

ROOM_CSV = REPO_ROOT / "data" / "draft_2025.csv"

#: Hard per-team QB cap for room bots: the 2025 room's observed maximum (one team took 4).
ROOM_QB_CAP = 4


def load_room_profile(path: Path = ROOM_CSV) -> dict[int, dict[str, float]]:
    """{round: {pos: probability}} from the real 150 picks. Comment lines start with '#'."""
    counts: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        rows = csv.DictReader(line for line in fh if not line.startswith("#"))
        for r in rows:
            rnd = int(r["round"])
            pos = r["pos"].upper()
            counts.setdefault(rnd, {})[pos] = counts.setdefault(rnd, {}).get(pos, 0) + 1
    profile: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for rnd, by_pos in counts.items():
        total = sum(by_pos.values())
        profile[rnd] = {pos: n / total for pos, n in by_pos.items()}
    return profile


def _room_bot_pick(rng, available, resolved, team_slot, pick_no, have, cfg, run, profile):
    """Sample a position from the room's real per-round frequencies, then a player at that
    position via the standard ADP softmax. Falls back to the ADP bot when every profiled
    position is illegal (hard constraint) or empty on the board."""
    rnd = snake.round_of(cfg.teams, pick_no)
    dist = profile.get(rnd) or profile[max(profile)]

    allowed = opp.hard_constraint_positions(have, cfg)
    on_board = {resolved[p.player_id][2] for p in available}
    legal = {}
    for pos, w in dist.items():
        if pos not in on_board:
            continue
        if allowed is not None and pos not in allowed:
            continue
        if pos == "QB" and have.get("QB", 0) >= ROOM_QB_CAP:
            continue
        legal[pos] = w
    if not legal:
        return _bot_pick(rng, available, resolved, team_slot, pick_no, have, cfg, run)

    positions = list(legal)
    weights = np.array([legal[p] for p in positions], dtype=float)
    weights = weights / weights.sum()
    pos_pick = positions[int(rng.choice(len(positions), p=weights))]

    pos_pool = [p for p in available if resolved[p.player_id][2] == pos_pick]
    # Within the position: the same softmax the ADP bot runs, with need/constraint state
    # blanked (the position decision has already been made by the empirical profile).
    return _bot_pick(rng, pos_pool, resolved, team_slot, pick_no, {}, cfg, run)


# ============================================================================== draft loop


def _bot_pick(rng, available, resolved, team_slot, pick_no, have, cfg, run):
    probs = opp.opponent_pick_probabilities(
        available, team_slot=team_slot, pick_no=pick_no, have=have, cfg=cfg, run=run, resolved=resolved
    )
    if not probs:  # defensive -- should not happen, opponents.py already falls back internally
        probs = {p.player_id: 1.0 for p in available}
    pids = list(probs.keys())
    p_arr = np.array([probs[pid] for pid in pids], dtype=float)
    p_arr = p_arr / p_arr.sum()
    return pids[int(rng.choice(len(pids), p=p_arr))]


def run_one_draft(
    *,
    seed: int,
    engine_slot: int | None,
    players_by_id: dict[str, BoardPlayer],
    full_players: list[BoardPlayer],
    resolved: dict[str, tuple[float, float | None, str]],
    cfg,
    n_sims: int,
    room_profile: dict[int, dict[str, float]] | None = None,
) -> dict[int, list[str]]:
    """One full 15-round, `cfg.teams`-team snake draft. `engine_slot=None` means every seat is
    the bot model (the baseline run); otherwise that one seat calls the real `recommend()`.
    `room_profile` switches every bot seat from the ADP model to the room-2025 bot.

    Returns {team_slot: [player_id, ...]} for every team's final roster.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    pool: dict[str, BoardPlayer] = dict(players_by_id)
    have: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {t: {} for t in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
    rosters: dict[int, list[str]] = {t: [] for t in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
    run = PositionalRun()
    # `LeagueConfig` has no separate "rounds" field -- `data/league_manual.yaml`'s own
    # `rounds: 15` IS `cfg.roster_size` (every roster spot gets drafted exactly once: 9
    # starters + 1 flex + 6 bench = 15 = 15 rounds), so that is the round count everywhere here.
    rounds = cfg.roster_size
    state = DraftState(teams=cfg.teams, rounds=rounds, my_slot=engine_slot or 1)

    total_picks = cfg.teams * rounds
    for pick_no in range(1, total_picks + 1):
        slot = snake.slot_on_clock(cfg.teams, pick_no)
        available = list(pool.values())
        if not available:
            break

        if slot == engine_slot:
            state.current_pick = pick_no
            state.my_slot = slot
            rec = recommend(state, cfg, full_players, n_sims=n_sims, seed=seed * 100_000 + pick_no)
            chosen = rec.candidates[0].player_id if rec.candidates else max(available, key=lambda p: p.dv).player_id
        elif room_profile is not None:
            chosen = _room_bot_pick(
                rng, available, resolved, slot, pick_no, have[slot], cfg, run, room_profile
            )
        else:
            chosen = _bot_pick(rng, available, resolved, slot, pick_no, have[slot], cfg, run)

        pos = pool[chosen].pos
        pool.pop(chosen)
        have[slot][pos] = have[slot].get(pos, 0) + 1
        rosters[slot].append(chosen)
        state.picks[pick_no] = Pick(pick_no=pick_no, team_slot=slot, player_id=chosen)
        run.observe(pos, remaining=list(pool.values()))

    return rosters


# ==================================================================================== main


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument("--engine-reps", type=int, default=15, help="engine-seat drafts PER SLOT")
    ap.add_argument(
        "--baseline-reps", type=int, default=1000,
        help="bot-only (all 10 seats) drafts -- each rep yields one baseline sample for EVERY slot",
    )
    ap.add_argument("--n-sims", type=int, default=25, help="recommend()'s internal Monte Carlo trial count")
    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=2026)
    ap.add_argument(
        "--room", choices=("adp", "room2025"), default="adp",
        help="opponent room: 'adp' = the standard ADP/need/herd bot model; 'room2025' = bots "
             "whose positional pacing is sampled from this league's real 2025 draft "
             "(6 first-round QBs, the rounds-5-6 QB run, 3+ QBs per team)",
    )
    args = ap.parse_args()

    room_profile = load_room_profile() if args.room == "room2025" else None

    logging.getLogger("draftroom.prep.crosswalk").setLevel(logging.ERROR)

    print("=" * 100)
    print(f"MOCK DRAFT: engine vs. {'room-2025' if room_profile else 'ADP-following'} bots, every draft slot 1-10")
    print("=" * 100)

    real = build_real_board()
    cfg = real.cfg
    players_by_id = {p.player_id: p for p in real.players}
    full_players = list(real.players)
    resolved = {p.player_id: (p.adp, p.stdev, p.pos) for p in real.players}

    print(
        f"\nboard: {len(full_players)} real players (real season projections x real FFC ADP), "
        f"league: {cfg.teams} teams, starters={dict(cfg.starters)}, flex={cfg.flex_slots}x"
        f"{sorted(cfg.flex_eligible)}, rounds={cfg.roster_size} (roster_size)"
    )
    print(f"objective: sum of real dv across the OPTIMAL starting lineup (see module docstring)")
    print(
        f"bots: draftroom.draft.opponents.opponent_pick_probabilities "
        f"(LeagueCalibration.national_only(), live PositionalRun herd detection)"
    )
    print(f"engine: draftroom.draft.recommend.recommend(n_sims={args.n_sims}), top candidate taken each turn")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ baseline: bot-only drafts
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    baseline_by_slot: dict[int, list[float]] = {s: [] for s in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
    for i in range(args.baseline_reps):
        rosters = run_one_draft(
            seed=args.seed + i,
            engine_slot=None,
            players_by_id=players_by_id,
            full_players=full_players,
            resolved=resolved,
            cfg=cfg,
            n_sims=args.n_sims,
            room_profile=room_profile,
        )
        fill = waiver_fill_for_draft(rosters, players_by_id, cfg)
        for slot, ids in rosters.items():
            baseline_by_slot[slot].append(
                starting_lineup_value(ids, players_by_id, cfg, waiver_fill=fill)
            )
        if (i + 1) % max(1, args.baseline_reps // 10) == 0:
            print(f"  baseline draft {i + 1}/{args.baseline_reps} done ({time.perf_counter() - t0:.1f}s elapsed)")
    baseline_elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t0
    print(f"baseline: {args.baseline_reps} bot-only drafts in {baseline_elapsed:.1f}s "
          f"({baseline_elapsed / max(1, args.baseline_reps):.4f}s/draft) -> "
          f"{args.baseline_reps} samples per slot")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------- engine: per-slot reps
    engine_by_slot: dict[int, list[float]] = {s: [] for s in range(1, cfg.teams + 1)}
    t1 = time.perf_counter()
    for slot in range(1, cfg.teams + 1):
        slot_t0 = time.perf_counter()
        for i in range(args.engine_reps):
            rosters = run_one_draft(
                seed=args.seed + 10_000 * slot + i,
                engine_slot=slot,
                players_by_id=players_by_id,
                full_players=full_players,
                resolved=resolved,
                cfg=cfg,
                n_sims=args.n_sims,
                room_profile=room_profile,
            )
            fill = waiver_fill_for_draft(rosters, players_by_id, cfg)
            engine_by_slot[slot].append(
                starting_lineup_value(rosters[slot], players_by_id, cfg, waiver_fill=fill)
            )
        print(
            f"  slot {slot:2d}: {args.engine_reps} engine drafts in {time.perf_counter() - slot_t0:.1f}s"
        )
    engine_elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t1
    print(f"engine: {cfg.teams} slots x {args.engine_reps} reps = "
          f"{cfg.teams * args.engine_reps} drafts in {engine_elapsed:.1f}s")

    # =================================================================================== report
    print("\n" + "=" * 100)
    print(f"PER-SLOT RESULT  (pass bar: engine median >= {PASS_PERCENTILE:.0f}th pct of the bot-only baseline)")
    print("=" * 100)
    header = (
        f"{'slot':>4}  {'baseline (bot-only), n='+str(args.baseline_reps):>28}  "
        f"{'engine, n='+str(args.engine_reps):>26}  {'engine pct in baseline':>22}  {'verdict':>8}"
    )
    print(header)
    print("-" * len(header))

    n_pass = 0
    for slot in range(1, cfg.teams + 1):
        base = np.array(baseline_by_slot[slot])
        eng = np.array(engine_by_slot[slot])
        base_mean, base_std = base.mean(), base.std(ddof=1)
        eng_mean, eng_std = eng.mean(), eng.std(ddof=1)
        eng_median = float(np.median(eng))

        pct = 100.0 * float((base <= eng_median).mean())
        # Per-rep percentile too, to show the engine's OWN spread against the same baseline
        # (the "noise level" the report has to state) rather than just one aggregate number.
        per_rep_pct = [100.0 * float((base <= v).mean()) for v in eng]

        verdict = "PASS" if pct >= PASS_PERCENTILE else "FAIL"
        n_pass += verdict == "PASS"
        print(
            f"{slot:>4}  {base_mean:9.1f} +/- {base_std:6.1f}          "
            f"{eng_mean:9.1f} +/- {eng_std:6.1f}         "
            f"{pct:8.1f}th (median)      {verdict:>8}"
        )
        print(
            f"      per-rep engine percentile range: [{min(per_rep_pct):.1f}, {max(per_rep_pct):.1f}]"
            f"th, mean {sum(per_rep_pct) / len(per_rep_pct):.1f}th  "
            f"(engine noise: std/sqrt(n)={eng_std / (len(eng) ** 0.5):.2f} pts on a base mean "
            f"of {base_mean:.1f})"
        )

    print("-" * len(header))
    print(f"\n{n_pass}/{cfg.teams} slots clear the {PASS_PERCENTILE:.0f}th-percentile bar.")
    return 0 if n_pass == cfg.teams else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
