import type { DraftState, Recommendation, SearchResponse } from "./types";

// Same-origin only, by design: the server binds 127.0.0.1 and this bundle is served from it.
// No absolute URL, no CDN, nothing that could reach past localhost.

async function getJSON<T>(url: string): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(url);
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`${url} -> ${res.status}`);
  return (await res.json()) as T;
}

async function postJSON<T>(url: string, body: unknown): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body ?? {}),
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const detail = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
    throw new Error((detail as { detail?: string }).detail ?? `${url} -> ${res.status}`);
  }
  return (await res.json()) as T;
}

export function getState(): Promise<DraftState> {
  return getJSON<DraftState>("/api/state");
}

export function search(query: string, opts: { includeDrafted?: boolean; limit?: number } = {}): Promise<SearchResponse> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ q: query });
  if (opts.limit) params.set("limit", String(opts.limit));
  if (opts.includeDrafted) params.set("include_drafted", "true");
  return getJSON<SearchResponse>(`/api/search?${params.toString()}`);
}

export function draftPick(playerId: string, opts: { pickNo?: number; teamSlot?: number; rawQuery?: string } = {}): Promise<DraftState> {
  return postJSON<DraftState>("/api/pick", {
    player_id: playerId,
    pick_no: opts.pickNo ?? null,
    team_slot: opts.teamSlot ?? null,
    raw_query: opts.rawQuery ?? "",
  });
}

export function addStub(name: string, pos: string, opts: { pickNo?: number; teamSlot?: number } = {}): Promise<DraftState> {
  return postJSON<DraftState>("/api/stub", {
    name,
    pos,
    pick_no: opts.pickNo ?? null,
    team_slot: opts.teamSlot ?? null,
  });
}

export function undo(): Promise<DraftState> {
  return postJSON<DraftState>("/api/undo", {});
}

export function correctPick(pickNo: number, fix: { playerId?: string; stubName?: string; stubPos?: string }): Promise<DraftState> {
  return postJSON<DraftState>("/api/correct", {
    pick_no: pickNo,
    player_id: fix.playerId ?? null,
    stub_name: fix.stubName ?? null,
    stub_pos: fix.stubPos ?? null,
  });
}

export function voidPick(pickNo: number): Promise<DraftState> {
  return postJSON<DraftState>("/api/void", { pick_no: pickNo });
}

export function setClock(pickNo: number): Promise<DraftState> {
  return postJSON<DraftState>("/api/clock", { pick_no: pickNo });
}

export function getRecommendation(
  target: "clock" | "mine" = "clock",
  eliteQbRankCutoff?: number,
): Promise<Recommendation> {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ target });
  if (eliteQbRankCutoff !== undefined) params.set("elite_qb_rank_cutoff", String(eliteQbRankCutoff));
  return getJSON<Recommendation>(`/api/recommendation?${params.toString()}`);
}

export function openStateSocket(onMessage: (state: DraftState) => void): WebSocket {
  const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss" : "ws";
  const ws = new WebSocket(`${proto}://${window.location.host}/ws`);
  ws.onmessage = (evt) => {
    try {
      onMessage(JSON.parse(evt.data) as DraftState);
    } catch {
      // A malformed push shouldn't take the socket down; the next /api/state poll (or the
      // next successful push) will resync.
    }
  };
  return ws;
}

1:@echo off
2:rem ============================================================================
3:rem DraftNight.bat -- launch the offline live draft assistant.
4:rem
5:rem Starts the draftroom server in --draft mode (installs and verifies the
6:rem socket guard: no outbound non-localhost connection is possible), waits for
7:rem its health endpoint, then opens it in the default browser in "app mode"
8:rem (no tabs/address bar). Everything here is local: no network call is made
9:rem by this script itself, and the server refuses to start if it cannot prove
10:rem it is offline-only. Safe to run with wifi physically off.
11:rem ============================================================================
12:setlocal enabledelayedexpansion
13:cd /d "%~dp0"
14:
15:set "PORT=8484"
16:set "VENV_PY=%~dp0.venv\Scripts\python.exe"
17:set "URL=http://127.0.0.1:%PORT%"
18:
19:if not exist "%VENV_PY%" (
20:    echo [DraftNight] Could not find the virtualenv Python at "%VENV_PY%".
21:    echo [DraftNight] Create it first: python -m venv .venv ^&^& .venv\Scripts\pip install -e .
22:    pause
23:    exit /b 1
24:)
25:
26:rem Draft mode refuses to start without a slot (deliberate: no silent slot-1
27:rem assumption). Ask for it here so draft night is one prompt, not a hang.
28::askslot
29:set "MYSLOT="
30:set /p "MYSLOT=[DraftNight] Enter your draft slot (1-10): "
31:echo %MYSLOT%| findstr /r "^[0-9][0-9]*$" >nul || goto :badslot
32:if %MYSLOT% GEQ 1 if %MYSLOT% LEQ 10 goto :slotok
33::badslot
34:echo [DraftNight] Please enter a whole number from 1 to 10.
35:goto :askslot
36::slotok
37:
38:echo [DraftNight] Starting the server (offline draft mode, slot %MYSLOT%) on %URL% ...
39:start "draftroom server" /min "%VENV_PY%" -m draftroom.server --draft --port %PORT% --my-slot %MYSLOT%
40:
41:echo [DraftNight] Waiting for the health endpoint to come up...
42:set /a TRIES=0
43:
44::waitloop
45:set /a TRIES+=1
46:powershell -NoProfile -Command ^
47:    "try { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -Uri '%URL%/healthz' -UseBasicParsing -TimeoutSec 1; exit ($(if ($r.StatusCode -eq 200) {0} else {1})) } catch { exit 1 }"
48:if not errorlevel 1 goto :ready
49:
50:if %TRIES% GEQ 40 (
51:    echo [DraftNight] Server did not respond after 20 seconds.
52:    echo [DraftNight] Check the minimized "draftroom server" window for errors.
53:    goto :openanyway
54:)
55:timeout /t 1 /nobreak >nul
56:goto :waitloop
57:
58::ready
59:echo [DraftNight] Server is up.
60:
61::openanyway
62:echo [DraftNight] Opening %URL% in app mode...
63:
64:set "CHROME=%ProgramFiles%\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"
65:if exist "%CHROME%" (
66:    start "" "%CHROME%" --app=%URL% --new-window
67:    goto :done
68:)
69:set "CHROME=%ProgramFiles(x86)%\Google\Chrome\Application\chrome.exe"
70:if exist "%CHROME%" (
71:    start "" "%CHROME%" --app=%URL% --new-window
72:    goto :done
73:)
74:set "EDGE=%ProgramFiles(x86)%\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"
75:if exist "%EDGE%" (
76:    start "" "%EDGE%" --app=%URL%
77:    goto :done
78:)
79:set "EDGE=%ProgramFiles%\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe"
80:if exist "%EDGE%" (
81:    start "" "%EDGE%" --app=%URL%
82:    goto :done
83:)

86:start "" "%URL%"
87:
88::done

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ fix "C" (c): VONA


class TestVonaInRanking:
    def test_cliff_position_outranks_deep_position_at_equal_dv(self):
        cfg = make_cfg()
        state = _state(current_pick=21)
        give_roster(state, 1, ["QB", "QB"])  # silence floor and grab
        players = [
            # RB cliff: r0 then a canyon, and r0's ADP says he's gone well before pick 40.
            player("r0", "RB", 22.0, 50.0, stdev=2.0),
            player("r1", "RB", 60.0, 5.0, stdev=2.0),
            player("r2", "RB", 61.0, 4.0, stdev=2.0),
            # WR depth: w0's twin survives, and w0 himself isn't leaving soon.
            player("w0", "WR", 80.0, 50.0, stdev=2.0),
            player("w1", "WR", 81.0, 49.0, stdev=2.0),
            player("w2", "WR", 82.0, 48.0, stdev=2.0),
            # bodies so every position exists
            player("q0", "QB", 90.0, 5.0),
            player("q1", "QB", 91.0, 4.0),
            player("t0", "TE", 95.0, 5.0),
            player("t1", "TE", 96.0, 4.0),
        ]
        rec = recommend(state, cfg, players, n_sims=30, seed=11, elite_qb_rank_cutoff=0)
        order = [c.player_id for c in rec.candidates]
        assert order.index("r0") < order.index("w0")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ gated opponent knobs


class TestGatedOpponentKnobs:
    def test_qb_mu_curve_identity_when_empty(self):
        calib = opp.LeagueCalibration.national_only()
        assert calib.remap_qb_mu(37.5) == 37.5

    def test_qb_mu_curve_interpolates_and_extends(self):
        calib = opp.LeagueCalibration(qb_mu_curve=((10.0, 20.0), (30.0, 60.0)))
        assert calib.remap_qb_mu(20.0) == 40.0  # midpoint
        assert calib.remap_qb_mu(5.0) == 15.0  # left tail keeps the +10 offset
        assert calib.remap_qb_mu(40.0) == 70.0  # right tail keeps the +30 offset

    def test_satiation_damps_only_complete_nonflex_positions(self):
        cfg = make_cfg()
        pool = [player("qX", "QB", 10.0, 5.0), player("rX", "RB", 11.0, 5.0)]
        have = {"QB": 2, "RB": 0}
        base = opp.opponent_scores(
            pool, team_slot=2, pick_no=30, have=have, cfg=cfg,
            calibration=opp.LeagueCalibration.national_only(),
        )
        damped = opp.opponent_scores(
            pool, team_slot=2, pick_no=30, have=have, cfg=cfg,
            calibration=opp.LeagueCalibration(satiation_damper=8.0),
        )
        assert damped["qX"] < base["qX"]  # QB-complete team feels less QB pull
        assert damped["rX"] == base["rX"]  # incomplete flex position untouched

I’m finishing the mutation-path and UI refresh audit now.

Completed the read-only review. The environment lacks a working Python executable and git/rg, so validation was by direct file inspection and cross-checking the submitted test logic rather than rerunning pytest.
  ✓ Inspect repository status and core draft state/event-log/snake implementations
  ✓ Review scarcity forcing, VONA, and opponent-model arithmetic
  ✓ Review expected-games, disagreement join, bonus/board pipeline, and new sim modes
  ✓ Review server/UI mutation paths and security boundaries
  ✓ Run targeted tests/static checks and produce ranked findings
codex
Bottom line: do not ship this batch yet. The core algorithms are mostly coherent, but draft night does not run the validated model that produced the 10/10 simulation result, and several durable-state paths can accept invalid events.

## CRITICAL

- **Draft mode bypasses the snapshot and real valuation pipeline.** `create_app()` loads raw cached data through `live_data.load_player_pool()`, not the newest immutable, reconciliation-approved snapshot. It then converts every player into a `BoardPlayer` using the ADP placeholder as `dv` and hardcodes `dv_sd=0.0`. [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:458), [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:479), [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:593)

  Consequences:

  - Bonus scoring, rank-conditioned games, real EVoB, and disagreement sigma do not affect draft-night recommendations.
  - The 10/10 simulation uses `build_real_board()`, so it is not exercising the model actually served by FastAPI.
  - All 980 players, including `is_ranked=False` players, are handed to `recommend()`. Once ranked depth is exhausted, zero-value “no projection” players enter candidate generation and can be recommended, contrary to the league contract. [recommend.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/recommend.py:443), [recommend.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/recommend.py:458)

## HIGH

- **The invariant suite weakened a non-negotiable gate so a known-bad result passes.** The contract requires 10–14 QBs in the top 30. The new check only requires the 2-QB count to exceed the 1-QB count; its own documentation says the real result is four QBs. That is precisely the normal-league-looking output the contract says must fail. [invariants.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/validate/invariants.py:87), [invariants.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/validate/invariants.py:117)

  The seventh expected-games check also runs against `deep_synthetic_pool()`, not the actual board. It therefore cannot detect that all real board seasons carry explicit games overrides. [invariants.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/validate/invariants.py:343), [invariants.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/validate/invariants.py:360)

- **The projection board bypasses both the expected-games curves and ESPN as source of record.** `build_real_board()` excludes any Sleeper projection with zero games, then sets every included player’s `expected_games` directly to Sleeper’s `games`. That bypasses `EXPECTED_GAMES_CURVE`, which only applies when the value is `None`. ESPN and FantasyPros are used only for disagreement. [board.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/validate/board.py:153), [board.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/validate/board.py:181), [replacement.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/valuation/replacement.py:306)

  Thus the new curves do not affect the projection board, mock-draft results, or draft-night server. The invariant suite does not reveal this.

- **Mutation endpoints can fsync impossible or duplicate state into the append-only log before validation.** The request models use unconstrained integers and strings. `/api/correct` does not reject an already-drafted or unknown player, `/api/pick` accepts an unknown player or an occupied `pick_no`, and `/api/clock` accepts zero, negative, or post-draft picks. [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:124), [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:645), [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:667), [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:681)

  A `clock_set` of zero is durably appended, then replay sets `current_pick=0`; subsequent serialization fails in snake arithmetic. A repeated `pick` silently replaces the earlier pick during replay rather than being represented as a correction. [state.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/state.py:145), [state.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/state.py:175), [events.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/events.py:73)

- **The scarcity floor can force QB while supply is actually adequate.** The trigger holds leaguewide unfilled demand constant while assuming every intervening opponent pick consumes supply. In reality, an opponent filling a QB starter reduces supply and demand together. For example, 21 startable QBs, 20 open slots, and an 18-pick gap forces QB immediately even though starter-needy QB selections preserve the one-player cushion. [recommend.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/recommend.py:503), [recommend.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/recommend.py:519)

  It also defines “startable” as `dv > 0`, whereas the tournament uses a QB-rank cutoff. With the live server’s `300 - ADP` placeholder, nearly every ranked QB is positive, so the validated tournament trigger and live trigger operate on different supply counts.

## MEDIUM

- **Void and correction leave the recommendation panel stale.** Recommendation fetching depends only on `current_pick`, mode, and the elite cutoff. Voiding or correcting a pick changes availability and rosters without changing `current_pick`, so the recommendation is not recomputed. [App.tsx](C:/dev/draftroom/frontend/src/App.tsx:61), [App.tsx](C:/dev/draftroom/frontend/src/App.tsx:65), [RosterPanel.tsx](C:/dev/draftroom/frontend/src/components/RosterPanel.tsx:30)

- **The demand clock uses the current turn rather than the following turn when Marc is on the clock.** `TurnContext.next_pick` equals `current_pick` on Marc’s turn, so the UI reports zero opponent picks. At slot 1, pick 1, it should show the 18 opponent picks before pick 20. The test currently enshrines the incorrect zero. [server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/server.py:316), [test_server.py](C:/dev/draftroom/tests/test_server.py:378)

- **VONA and pair-survival arithmetic include one impossible draft opportunity.** Once Marc chooses candidate X, every other player is guaranteed to survive Marc’s current pick. The code conditions survival from `state.current_pick`, so it prices the possibility that an opponent takes a player at Marc’s current selection. At a true back-to-back turn, partner survival should be exactly 100%, but this code returns `S(current+1)/S(current) < 1`. Now that VONA moves rankings, this formerly display-only off-by-one affects decisions. [recommend.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/recommend.py:572), [recommend.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/recommend.py:591), [vona.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/draft/vona.py:71)

## LOW

- **The disagreement enrichment key can silently collide.** The current join is generally safe because both sides use the same FFC name/team fields, but `clean_name(name)|team` excludes position and silently overwrites duplicate keys. Add position and assert uniqueness. [live_data.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/live_data.py:125), [live_data.py](C:/dev/draftroom/backend/draftroom/live_data.py:163)

- **`DraftNight.bat` hardcodes 10 teams and expands untrusted prompt text as batch syntax before validation.** This conflicts with the runtime-config rule and permits metacharacter execution from the prompt. Current local-only use limits exposure. [DraftNight.bat](C:/dev/draftroom/DraftNight.bat:30), [DraftNight.bat](C:/dev/draftroom/DraftNight.bat:31)

The append/fsync/replay mechanics themselves look sound for valid events, and the central snake mapping is correct. I could not independently rerun pytest because this review environment has no working Python executable; the findings above come from direct code-path inspection.
